        if(typeof window.selectClipForChrome==='function')window.selectClipForChrome(id);
        if(typeof window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel==='function')window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel('font');
        else if(typeof window.openEthvelleTextComposer==='function')window.openEthvelleTextComposer(id);
      });
      layer.appendChild(btn);
    }
    return btn;
  }
  function positionTextEditHandle(){
    const btn=ensureTextEditHandle(),layer=$('selBoxLayer'),stage=$('canvasStage');if(!btn||!layer||!stage)return;
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':selectedClip);
    const ov=Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===id):null;
    if(!ov||ov.type!=='text') {btn.style.display='none';return;}
    let tg=null;try{tg=typeof window.selBoxTarget==='function'?window.selBoxTarget():(typeof selBoxTarget==='function'?selBoxTarget():null);}catch(_e){}
    if(!tg||tg.kind!=='overlay') {btn.style.display='none';return;}
    let g=null;try{g=typeof selBoxGeom==='function'?selBoxGeom(tg):null;}catch(_e){}
    if(!g){btn.style.display='none';return;}
    const a=(Number(g.rot)||0)*Math.PI/180,cs=Math.cos(a),sn=Math.sin(a);
    const px=g.cx+(g.w/2)*cs+(g.h/2)*sn,py=g.cy+(g.w/2)*sn-(g.h/2)*cs;
    const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect(),edge=19;
    const clientX=clamp(r.left+px,edge,Math.max(edge,window.innerWidth-edge));
    const clientY=clamp(r.top+py,edge,Math.max(edge,window.innerHeight-edge));
    btn.style.left=(clientX-r.left)+'px';btn.style.top=(clientY-r.top)+'px';btn.style.display='flex';
  }
  const originalUpdate=window.updateSelBox;
  if(typeof originalUpdate==='function'&&!originalUpdate.__ethvelleV15Pencil){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=originalUpdate.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(positionTextEditHandle);return result;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV15Pencil=true;window.updateSelBox=wrapped;
  }
  const originalSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof originalSelect==='function'&&!originalSelect.__ethvelleV15Pencil){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=originalSelect.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(positionTextEditHandle);return result;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV15Pencil=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }
  const originalDeselect=window.deselectAllClips;
  if(typeof originalDeselect==='function'&&!originalDeselect.__ethvelleV15Pencil){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=originalDeselect.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(positionTextEditHandle);return result;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV15Pencil=true;window.deselectAllClips=wrapped;
  }
  function boot(){
    ensureTextEditHandle();positionTextEditHandle();
    window.addEventListener('resize',positionTextEditHandle,{passive:true});
    document.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>requestAnimationFrame(positionTextEditHandle),true);
    const mo=new MutationObserver(()=>{ensureTextEditHandle();requestAnimationFrame(positionTextEditHandle);});
    if(document.body)mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>



<!-- Ethvelle editor polish v16: separate corner actions, thumb-only sliders, shared picker + magnifying eyedropper -->

<script id="ethvelle-text-fixes-v16-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));

  // Add a dedicated Move button while keeping the existing resize/rotate corner available.
  function ensureMoveHandle(){
    const layer=$('selBoxLayer');if(!layer)return null;
    let btn=$('sbTextMove');
    if(!btn){
      btn=document.createElement('button');btn.type='button';btn.id='sbTextMove';btn.className='sb-btn sb-text-move';
      btn.setAttribute('aria-label','Move text');btn.setAttribute('title','Drag to move text');
      btn.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 3v18M3 12h18M12 3l-3 3M12 3l3 3M21 12l-3-3M21 12l-3 3M12 21l-3-3M12 21l3-3M3 12l3-3M3 12l3 3"/></svg>';
      layer.appendChild(btn);wireMoveHandle(btn);
    }
    return btn;
  }
  function selectedText(){
    try{
      const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':selectedClip);
      return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===id&&o.type==='text'):null;
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function getTarget(){try{return typeof window.selBoxTarget==='function'?window.selBoxTarget():(typeof selBoxTarget==='function'?selBoxTarget():null);}catch(_e){return null;}}
  function getGeometry(tg){try{return typeof window.selBoxGeom==='function'?window.selBoxGeom(tg):(typeof selBoxGeom==='function'?selBoxGeom(tg):null);}catch(_e){return null;}}
  function localCorner(g,sx,sy){
    const a=(Number(g.rot)||0)*Math.PI/180,cs=Math.cos(a),sn=Math.sin(a);
    return {x:g.cx+sx*g.w/2*cs-sy*g.h/2*sn,y:g.cy+sx*g.w/2*sn+sy*g.h/2*cs};
  }
  function placeCorner(el,pt,stageRect){
    if(!el)return;
    const edge=17;
    const cx=clamp(stageRect.left+pt.x,edge,Math.max(edge,window.innerWidth-edge));
    const cy=clamp(stageRect.top+pt.y,edge,Math.max(edge,window.innerHeight-edge));
    el.style.left=(cx-stageRect.left)+'px';el.style.top=(cy-stageRect.top)+'px';el.style.display='flex';
  }
  function layoutTextCorners(){
    const layer=$('selBoxLayer'),stage=$('canvasStage');if(!layer||!stage)return;
    const edit=$('sbTextEdit'),move=ensureMoveHandle(),scale=$('sbScale');
    const ov=selectedText(),tg=getTarget();
    if(!ov||!tg||tg.kind!=='overlay'){
      if(edit)edit.style.display='none';if(move)move.style.display='none';return;
    }
    const g=getGeometry(tg);if(!g){if(edit)edit.style.display='none';if(move)move.style.display='none';return;}
    const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();
    // Keep × at top-left (core editor); pencil at top-right; resize/rotate bottom-left; move bottom-right.
    if(edit)placeCorner(edit,localCorner(g,1,-1),r);
    if(scale)placeCorner(scale,localCorner(g,-1,1),r);
    if(move)placeCorner(move,localCorner(g,1,1),r);
  }
  /* v231: coalesce move-handle input to one frame and paint only the dragged text element. */
  function wireMoveHandle(btn){
    if(btn.dataset.evpaMoveWired==='1')return;btn.dataset.evpaMoveWired='1';
    let drag=null;
    const paint=(state,finishOnly)=>{
      state.raf=0;if(drag!==state)return;
      const dx=(state.lastX-state.startX)*state.sx,dy=(state.lastY-state.startY)*state.sy;
      if(!state.moved&&Math.hypot(dx,dy)<1.5)return;state.moved=true;
      state.ov.transform.offsetX=state.startOX+dx;state.ov.transform.offsetY=state.startOY+dy;
      if(!finishOnly){
        if(!window.ethvelleTextDragPaintFast(state.ov))try{if(typeof renderOverlayMedia==='function')renderOverlayMedia(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():0);}catch(_e){}
        try{if(typeof updateSelBox==='function')updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}
      }
    };
    btn.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
      const ov=selectedText(),stage=$('canvasStage');if(!ov||!ov.transform||!stage)return;
      const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();if(!r.width||!r.height)return;
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation?.();
      try{btn.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
      let logicalW=r.width,logicalH=r.height;
      try{if(typeof stageW!=='undefined'&&Number(stageW)>0)logicalW=Number(stageW);if(typeof stageH!=='undefined'&&Number(stageH)>0)logicalH=Number(stageH);}catch(_e){}
      drag={id:e.pointerId,ov,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,startOX:Number(ov.transform.offsetX)||0,startOY:Number(ov.transform.offsetY)||0,
        sx:logicalW/r.width,sy:logicalH/r.height,moved:false,lastX:e.clientX,lastY:e.clientY,raf:0};
      window.__evTextDragActive=true; /* v231: suppress redundant repair during handle drag. */
    },{passive:false});
    btn.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      if(!drag||drag.id!==e.pointerId)return;
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation?.();
      const state=drag;state.lastX=e.clientX;state.lastY=e.clientY;
      if(!state.raf)state.raf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>paint(state,false));
    },{passive:false});
    const finish=(e,cancelled)=>{
      if(!drag||drag.id!==e.pointerId)return;
      e.preventDefault?.();e.stopPropagation?.();e.stopImmediatePropagation?.();
      const state=drag;
      if(Number.isFinite(e.clientX)&&Number.isFinite(e.clientY)){state.lastX=e.clientX;state.lastY=e.clientY;}
      if(state.raf)cancelAnimationFrame(state.raf);state.raf=0;
      paint(state,true);drag=null;window.__evTextDragActive=false; /* v231: release drag suppression. */
      try{btn.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
      if(cancelled&&state.moved){state.ov.transform.offsetX=state.startOX;state.ov.transform.offsetY=state.startOY;}
      if(state.moved){
        /* v231: the full stage renderer is reserved for pointerup/cancel. */
        try{if(typeof renderOverlayMedia==='function')renderOverlayMedia(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():0);}catch(_e){}
        try{if(typeof updateSelBox==='function')updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}
        if(!cancelled){try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}catch(_e){}}
      }
    };
    btn.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>finish(e,false),{passive:false});
    btn.addEventListener('pointercancel',e=>finish(e,true),{passive:false});
    btn.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();},true);
  }

  const originalUpdateSelBox=window.updateSelBox;
  if(typeof originalUpdateSelBox==='function'&&!originalUpdateSelBox.__ethvelleV16Corners){
    const wrapped=function(){const out=originalUpdateSelBox.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners&&window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners());return out;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV16Corners=true;window.updateSelBox=wrapped;
  }
  const originalSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof originalSelect==='function'&&!originalSelect.__ethvelleV16Corners){
    const wrapped=function(){const out=originalSelect.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners&&window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners());return out;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV16Corners=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }
  const originalDeselect=window.deselectAllClips;
  if(typeof originalDeselect==='function'&&!originalDeselect.__ethvelleV16Corners){
    const wrapped=function(){const out=originalDeselect.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners&&window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners());return out;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV16Corners=true;window.deselectAllClips=wrapped;
  }
  function bootCorners(){
    ensureMoveHandle();
    window.addEventListener('resize',()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners&&window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners()),{passive:true});
    document.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners&&window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners()),true);
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners&&window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners());
  }

  // Picsart-like eyedropper: a circular live magnifier with a sampled-color rim and centered crosshair.
  function parseCssColor(value){
    const m=String(value||'').match(/rgba?\(\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*[, ]+\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*[, ]+\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)(?:\s*[,/]\s*([\d.]+%?))?\s*\)/i);
    if(!m)return null;const a=m[4]===undefined?1:(String(m[4]).includes('%')?parseFloat(m[4])/100:parseFloat(m[4]));if(a<=.025)return null;
    return '#'+m.slice(1,4).map(v=>Math.round(clamp(Number(v),0,255)).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
  }
  function pixelFromCanvas(el,x,y){
    try{const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();if(!r.width||!r.height||!el.width||!el.height)return null;
      const px=Math.max(0,Math.min(el.width-1,Math.floor((x-r.left)*el.width/r.width))),py=Math.max(0,Math.min(el.height-1,Math.floor((y-r.top)*el.height/r.height)));
      const d=el.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true})?.getImageData(px,py,1,1).data;if(!d||d[3]<8)return null;
      return '#'+[d[0],d[1],d[2]].map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function pixelFromMedia(el,x,y){
    try{
      const video=el.tagName==='VIDEO',nw=video?(el.videoWidth||0):(el.naturalWidth||0),nh=video?(el.videoHeight||0):(el.naturalHeight||0);
      if(!nw||!nh||video&&el.readyState<2)return null;
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();if(!r.width||!r.height)return null;
      let sx=(x-r.left)/r.width*nw,sy=(y-r.top)/r.height*nh;const fit=getComputedStyle(el).objectFit||'fill';
      if(fit==='cover'||fit==='contain'){
        const scale=fit==='cover'?Math.max(r.width/nw,r.height/nh):Math.min(r.width/nw,r.height/nh);
        sx=(x-r.left-(r.width-nw*scale)/2)/scale;sy=(y-r.top-(r.height-nh*scale)/2)/scale;
      }
      sx=clamp(Math.floor(sx),0,nw-1);sy=clamp(Math.floor(sy),0,nh-1);
      const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=1;const ctx=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});ctx.drawImage(el,sx,sy,1,1,0,0,1,1);const d=ctx.getImageData(0,0,1,1).data;
      if(d[3]<8)return null;return '#'+[d[0],d[1],d[2]].map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function colorAt(stage,x,y){
    const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();if(x<r.left||x>r.right||y<r.top||y>r.bottom)return null;
    let stack=[];try{stack=document.elementsFromPoint(x,y).filter(el=>el===stage||stage.contains(el));}catch(_e){}
    for(const el of stack){
      if(el.tagName==='CANVAS'){const c=pixelFromCanvas(el,x,y);if(c)return c;}
      if(el.tagName==='IMG'||el.tagName==='VIDEO'){const c=pixelFromMedia(el,x,y);if(c)return c;}
      if(el.classList?.contains('ethvelle-overlay-text-content')){const c=parseCssColor(getComputedStyle(el).color);if(c)return c;}
      const cs=getComputedStyle(el),c=parseCssColor(cs.backgroundColor);if(c&&Number(cs.opacity||1)>.025)return c;
    }
    return parseCssColor(getComputedStyle(stage).backgroundColor)||'#777777';
  }
  function copyScene(stage,viewport){
    const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect(),scene=stage.cloneNode(true);scene.classList.add('evpa-loupe-scene');
    const setImportant=(el,k,v)=>el.style.setProperty(k,v,'important');
    setImportant(scene,'position','absolute');setImportant(scene,'left','0px');setImportant(scene,'top','0px');
    setImportant(scene,'width',r.width+'px');setImportant(scene,'height',r.height+'px');setImportant(scene,'max-width','none');setImportant(scene,'max-height','none');
    setImportant(scene,'margin','0');setImportant(scene,'transform-origin','0 0');setImportant(scene,'overflow','hidden');setImportant(scene,'pointer-events','none');
    // Canvas pixels are not copied by cloneNode; copy the visible paint into the matching snapshot canvases.
    const srcCanvases=stage.querySelectorAll('canvas'),dstCanvases=scene.querySelectorAll('canvas');
    for(let i=0;i<Math.min(srcCanvases.length,dstCanvases.length);i++){
      const src=srcCanvases[i],dst=dstCanvases[i];try{dst.width=src.width;dst.height=src.height;dst.getContext('2d')?.drawImage(src,0,0,dst.width,dst.height);}catch(_e){}
    }
    // Snapshot video frames so the lens reflects the frame currently visible in the preview.
    const srcVideos=stage.querySelectorAll('video'),dstVideos=scene.querySelectorAll('video');
    for(let i=0;i<Math.min(srcVideos.length,dstVideos.length);i++){
      const src=srcVideos[i],dst=dstVideos[i];try{
        if(!src.videoWidth||!src.videoHeight||src.readyState<2)continue;
        const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=src.videoWidth;c.height=src.videoHeight;c.className=dst.className;
        const cs=getComputedStyle(src);['position','left','top','right','bottom','width','height','object-fit','object-position','transform','transform-origin','opacity','z-index','border-radius','display'].forEach(k=>{const v=cs.getPropertyValue(k);if(v)c.style.setProperty(k,v,'important');});
        c.getContext('2d')?.drawImage(src,0,0,c.width,c.height);dst.replaceWith(c);
      }catch(_e){}
    }
    viewport.appendChild(scene);return scene;
  }
  function sampleKey(){
    const fromTarget=typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget():'';
    const tab=$('evpaTabs')?.querySelector('.evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab||'color';
    const fromTab=tab==='stroke'?'strokeColor':tab==='shadow'?'shadowColor':(['highlight','background'].includes(tab)?'textBackgroundColor':'textColor');
    return ['textColor','strokeColor','shadowColor','textBackgroundColor'].includes(fromTarget)?fromTarget:fromTab;
  }
  window.ethvellePicsartSampleColor=function(){
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Canvas is not available');return;}
    if(window.__ethvelleV16Sampling)return;
    window.__ethvelleV16Sampling=true;
    const key=sampleKey(),picker=$('ethvelleColorPicker');const pickerWasOpen=!!picker?.classList.contains('active');
    const roots=[$('ethvelleTextComposer'),picker,$('ethvelleTextStylePanel'),$('ethvellePicsartTextDock')].filter(Boolean);
    const saved=roots.map(el=>({el,visibility:el.style.visibility}));roots.forEach(el=>el.style.visibility='hidden');
    let loupe=$('evpaSamplerLoupe');if(!loupe){loupe=document.createElement('div');loupe.id='evpaSamplerLoupe';document.body.appendChild(loupe);}
    loupe.innerHTML='<div class="evpa-loupe-viewport"><div class="evpa-loupe-crosshair"></div><i class="evpa-loupe-center"></i></div>';
    const viewport=loupe.querySelector('.evpa-loupe-viewport');const scene=copyScene(stage,viewport);loupe.style.display='block';
    let pointer=null,lastColor='#777777',stageRect=stage.getBoundingClientRect();const zoom=2.35;
    const updateLens=(x,y)=>{
      stageRect=stage.getBoundingClientRect();
      const lensW=loupe.getBoundingClientRect().width||158,lensH=loupe.getBoundingClientRect().height||158;
      const halfW=lensW/2,halfH=lensH/2;
      // Keep the magnifier fully on-screen near the preview edges while the crosshair remains
      // centered on the exact pixel currently under the finger.
      const lensX=clamp(x,Math.min(halfW+4,window.innerWidth/2),Math.max(Math.min(halfW+4,window.innerWidth/2),window.innerWidth-halfW-4));
      const lensY=clamp(y,Math.min(halfH+4,window.innerHeight/2),Math.max(Math.min(halfH+4,window.innerHeight/2),window.innerHeight-halfH-4));
      loupe.style.left=lensX+'px';loupe.style.top=lensY+'px';
      const color=colorAt(stage,x,y);if(color){lastColor=color;loupe.style.setProperty('--evpa-sample-color',color);}
      const vw=Math.max(40,viewport.clientWidth),vh=Math.max(40,viewport.clientHeight);
      scene.style.setProperty('left',(vw/2-(x-stageRect.left)*zoom)+'px','important');
      scene.style.setProperty('top',(vh/2-(y-stageRect.top)*zoom)+'px','important');
      scene.style.setProperty('transform','scale('+zoom+')','important');
      return color;
    };
    updateLens(window.innerWidth/2,window.innerHeight/2);
    const inside=(x,y)=>{const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();return x>=r.left&&x<=r.right&&y>=r.top&&y<=r.bottom;};
    const stop=e=>{e.preventDefault?.();e.stopPropagation?.();e.stopImmediatePropagation?.();};
    const begin=e=>{
      if(pointer!==null||!inside(e.clientX,e.clientY))return;
      pointer=e.pointerId;stop(e);try{e.target?.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}updateLens(e.clientX,e.clientY);
    };
    const move=e=>{if(pointer===null||e.pointerId!==pointer)return;stop(e);updateLens(e.clientX,e.clientY);};
    const cleanup=()=>{
      document.removeEventListener('pointerdown',begin,true);document.removeEventListener('pointermove',move,true);document.removeEventListener('pointerup',end,true);document.removeEventListener('pointercancel',cancel,true);
      loupe.style.display='none';viewport.replaceChildren();saved.forEach(o=>o.el.style.visibility=o.visibility);window.__ethvelleV16Sampling=false;
    };
    const end=e=>{
      if(pointer===null||e.pointerId!==pointer)return;stop(e);const x=e.clientX,y=e.clientY;const sampled=inside(x,y)?colorAt(stage,x,y):null;cleanup();
      if(sampled&&typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColor==='function'){
        window.ethvellePicsartSetColor(key,sampled);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Canvas color applied',{duration:1100});
      }else if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Pick a visible color inside the canvas',{duration:1500});
      if(sampled&&pickerWasOpen&&typeof window.openEthvelleColorPicker==='function')requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.openEthvelleColorPicker(key));
    };
    const cancel=e=>{if(pointer!==null&&e.pointerId===pointer){stop(e);cleanup();}};
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown',begin,{capture:true,passive:false});document.addEventListener('pointermove',move,{capture:true,passive:false});
    document.addEventListener('pointerup',end,{capture:true,passive:false});document.addEventListener('pointercancel',cancel,{capture:true,passive:false});
  };

  function boot(){ensureMoveHandle();requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners&&window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners());}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-editor-repair-v17-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(n,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,n));
  const rangeScope='#ethvellePicsartTextDock,#ethvelleTextStylePanel,#ethvelleTextComposer,#evpaCustomEffectEditor,#ethvelleColorPicker,#textPanelBody';
  const rangeSelector=rangeScope.split(',').map(s=>s+' input[type="range"]').join(',');
  const shields=new Map();
  let rangeDrag=null;
  const stop=e=>{if(!e)return;e.preventDefault?.();e.stopPropagation?.();e.stopImmediatePropagation?.();};
  function isVisible(el){
    if(!el||!el.isConnected)return false;
    let n=el;
    while(n&&n!==document.documentElement){
      const cs=getComputedStyle(n);
      if(cs.display==='none'||cs.visibility==='hidden'||Number(cs.opacity)===0||n.hidden)return false;
      n=n.parentElement;
    }
    const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();return r.width>0&&r.height>0&&r.bottom>=0&&r.top<=window.innerHeight&&r.right>=0&&r.left<=window.innerWidth;
  }
  function geometry(input){
    const r=input.getBoundingClientRect();
    const h=Math.max(10,Math.min(18,(r.height||24)*.46));
    const left=r.left+h,right=Math.max(left+1,r.right-h);
    const min=Number.isFinite(parseFloat(input.min))?parseFloat(input.min):0;
    const max=Number.isFinite(parseFloat(input.max))?parseFloat(input.max):100;
    const value=Number.isFinite(parseFloat(input.value))?parseFloat(input.value):min;
    let fraction=max===min?0:clamp((value-min)/(max-min),0,1);
    const rtl=getComputedStyle(input).direction==='rtl';if(rtl)fraction=1-fraction;
    return {r,left,right,min,max,value,step:input.step==='any'?0:(Number(input.step)||1),thumbX:left+fraction*(right-left),thumbHalf:h,rtl};
  }
  function dispatch(input,type){input.dispatchEvent(new Event(type,{bubbles:true}));}
  function setFromX(input,x){
    const g=geometry(input);let fraction=clamp((x-g.left)/(g.right-g.left),0,1);if(g.rtl)fraction=1-fraction;
    let value=g.min+fraction*(g.max-g.min);if(g.step>0)value=g.min+Math.round((value-g.min)/g.step)*g.step;
    value=clamp(value,g.min,g.max);
    const decimals=(String(input.step||'1').split('.')[1]||'').length;
    const next=String(Number(value.toFixed(Math.min(6,decimals))));
    if(input.value!==next){input.value=next;dispatch(input,'input');}
  }
  function refreshShields(){
    /* Keep the actual circular native thumb as the only touch target. No invisible
       shield nodes are created, measured, or moved during scrolling. */
    document.querySelectorAll(rangeSelector).forEach(input=>{
      /* v191: text-panel sliders are driven by the row-level controller (ethvelle-v191-text-slider-js);
         the native input must stay out of hit-testing so a swipe never needs to land on the thumb. */
      input.style.setProperty('pointer-events','none','important');
      input.style.setProperty('touch-action','pan-y','important');
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('.evpa-range-interceptor').forEach(el=>el.remove());
    shields.clear();
  }
  window.__ethvelleRefreshRangeOverlays=refreshShields;
  function onRangeDown(e){
    const shield=e.target?.closest?.('.evpa-range-interceptor');if(!shield)return;
    const input=shield.__evpaRangeInput;if(!input||input.disabled){stop(e);return;}
    const g=geometry(input);
    // Do not cancel at pointerdown. With touch-action:pan-y the browser can still recognize
    // an up/down scroll gesture; a horizontal gesture is claimed only after its intent is clear.
    const scrollTarget=input.closest('#evpaControls, .evts-panel, .evtc-dock, .evce-sheet, .evcp-sheet, .evpa-dock');
    rangeDrag={id:e.pointerId,input,shield,scrollTarget,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,lastX:e.clientX,lastY:e.clientY,
      startValue:String(input.value),startScrollTop:scrollTarget?scrollTarget.scrollTop:0,moved:false,mode:'pending',onThumb:Math.abs(e.clientX-g.thumbX)<=Math.max(15,g.thumbHalf+3)&&Math.abs(e.clientY-(g.r.top+g.r.height/2))<=Math.max(20,g.r.height/2+8)};
    e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    try{shield.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
  }
  function onRangeMove(e){
    const d=rangeDrag;if(!d||d.id!==e.pointerId)return;
    d.lastX=e.clientX;d.lastY=e.clientY;
    if(d.mode==='pending'){
      const dx=Math.abs(d.lastX-d.startX),dy=Math.abs(d.lastY-d.startY);
      if(Math.max(dx,dy)<4)return;
      if(dy>dx*1.12){d.mode='scroll';stop(e);if(d.scrollTarget)d.scrollTarget.scrollTop=Math.max(0,Math.min(d.scrollTarget.scrollHeight-d.scrollTarget.clientHeight,d.startScrollTop-(d.lastY-d.startY)));return;}
      if(dx>dy*1.12){d.mode='value';stop(e);d.moved=true;setFromX(d.input,d.lastX);return;}
      return;
    }
    if(d.mode==='scroll'){stop(e);if(d.scrollTarget)d.scrollTarget.scrollTop=Math.max(0,Math.min(d.scrollTarget.scrollHeight-d.scrollTarget.clientHeight,d.startScrollTop-(d.lastY-d.startY)));return;}
    if(d.mode!=='value')return;
    stop(e);if(!d.moved&&Math.abs(d.lastX-d.startX)<2)return;d.moved=true;setFromX(d.input,d.lastX);
  }
  function finishRange(e,cancelled){
    const d=rangeDrag;if(!d||d.id!==e.pointerId)return;
    rangeDrag=null;
    if(d.mode==='scroll'||d.mode==='pending'){
      // A vertical scroll or a tap on the slider track must not be swallowed by the overlay.
      try{d.shield.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
      requestAnimationFrame(refreshShields);return;
    }
    stop(e);
    if(cancelled){if(d.input.value!==d.startValue){d.input.value=d.startValue;dispatch(d.input,'input');dispatch(d.input,'change');}}
    else if(d.moved){setFromX(d.input,d.lastX);dispatch(d.input,'change');}
    try{d.shield.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
    requestAnimationFrame(refreshShields);
  }
  window.addEventListener('pointerdown',onRangeDown,{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('pointermove',onRangeMove,{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>finishRange(e,false),{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('pointercancel',e=>finishRange(e,true),{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target?.closest?.('.evpa-range-interceptor'))stop(e);},{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('resize',refreshShields,{passive:true});
  // No global scroll listener: range shields are disabled, so rescanning every slider
  // during bottom-toolbar/timeline scrolling only adds main-thread work and touch stutter.
  document.addEventListener('transitionend',refreshShields,true);
  const rangeObserverGesturePointers=new Set();
  let rangeObserverRefreshPending=false;
  const rangeObserverGestureSurface='#canvasStage,.canvas-wrap,#timelineScroll,#bottomToolsDefault,#bottomToolsClip,#bottomToolsKeyframe';
  const flushRangeObserverRefresh=()=>{
    if(rangeObserverGesturePointers.size||!rangeObserverRefreshPending)return;
    rangeObserverRefreshPending=false;
    requestAnimationFrame(refreshShields);
  };
  const markRangeObserverGesture=e=>{
    if(e.pointerId==null||!e.target?.closest?.(rangeObserverGestureSurface))return;
    rangeObserverGesturePointers.add(e.pointerId);
  };
  const clearRangeObserverGesture=e=>{
    if(e.pointerId!=null)rangeObserverGesturePointers.delete(e.pointerId);
    flushRangeObserverRefresh();
  };
  window.addEventListener('pointerdown',markRangeObserverGesture,{capture:true,passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('pointerup',clearRangeObserverGesture,true);
  window.addEventListener('pointercancel',clearRangeObserverGesture,true);
  window.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',clearRangeObserverGesture,true);
  window.addEventListener('blur',()=>{rangeObserverGesturePointers.clear();flushRangeObserverRefresh();});
  const rangeMo=new MutationObserver(records=>{
    if(rangeObserverGesturePointers.size){rangeObserverRefreshPending=true;return;}
    const relevant=records.some(record=>{
      const target=record.target?.nodeType===1?record.target:record.target?.parentElement;
      if(!target||target.classList?.contains('evpa-range-interceptor'))return false;
      return !!(target.matches?.(rangeScope)||target.closest?.(rangeScope));
    });
    if(relevant)refreshShields();
  });
  function startRangeObserver(){
    refreshShields();
    if(document.body)rangeMo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','hidden','style']});
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',startRangeObserver,{once:true});else startRangeObserver();

  // Keep the edit pencil at top-right and the dedicated move button at bottom-right.
  function selectedText(){
    try{const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':selectedClip);return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===id&&o.type==='text'):null;}catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function currentTarget(){try{return typeof window.selBoxTarget==='function'?window.selBoxTarget():(typeof selBoxTarget==='function'?selBoxTarget():null);}catch(_e){return null;}}
  function geomFor(t){try{return typeof window.selBoxGeom==='function'?window.selBoxGeom(t):(typeof selBoxGeom==='function'?selBoxGeom(t):null);}catch(_e){return null;}}
  function corner(g,sx,sy){const a=(Number(g.rot)||0)*Math.PI/180,cs=Math.cos(a),sn=Math.sin(a);return{x:g.cx+sx*g.w/2*cs-sy*g.h/2*sn,y:g.cy+sx*g.w/2*sn+sy*g.h/2*cs};}
  function place(btn,p,r){if(!btn)return;const edge=18;const x=clamp(r.left+p.x,edge,Math.max(edge,innerWidth-edge)),y=clamp(r.top+p.y,edge,Math.max(edge,innerHeight-edge));btn.style.setProperty('left',(x-r.left)+'px','important');btn.style.setProperty('top',(y-r.top)+'px','important');btn.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');}
  function layoutCorners(){
    const layer=$('selBoxLayer'),stage=$('canvasStage');if(!layer||!stage)return;
    const edit=$('sbTextEdit'),move=$('sbTextMove'),scale=$('sbScale'),ov=selectedText();
    if(!ov){if(edit)edit.style.setProperty('display','none','important');if(move)move.style.setProperty('display','none','important');return;}
    const tg=currentTarget();
    if(!tg||tg.kind!=='overlay'){if(edit)edit.style.setProperty('display','none','important');if(move)move.style.setProperty('display','none','important');return;}
    const g=geomFor(tg);if(!g){if(edit)edit.style.setProperty('display','none','important');if(move)move.style.setProperty('display','none','important');return;}
    const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();place(edit,corner(g,1,-1),r);place(scale,corner(g,-1,1),r);place(move,corner(g,1,1),r);
  }
  window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners=layoutCorners;
  const safeLayout=()=>requestAnimationFrame(layoutCorners);
  window.addEventListener('resize',safeLayout,{passive:true});
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',safeLayout,true);
  // Position updates are driven by the wrapped updateSelBox/select/deselect functions,
  // resize and pointerup; no body-wide MutationObserver is needed while #sbScale is dragged.

  // Intercept the move handle before the editor's transform/resize handlers can see the gesture.
  let moveDrag=null;
  function onMoveDown(e){
    if(!e.target?.closest?.('#sbTextMove'))return;
    const ov=selectedText(),stage=$('canvasStage');if(!ov||!stage){stop(e);return;}
    if(!ov.transform)ov.transform={offsetX:0,offsetY:0};
    const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();if(!r.width||!r.height){stop(e);return;}
    stop(e);moveDrag={id:e.pointerId,btn:$('sbTextMove'),ov,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,
      ox:Number(ov.transform.offsetX)||0,oy:Number(ov.transform.offsetY)||0,
      sx:(typeof stageW!=='undefined'&&Number(stageW)>0?Number(stageW):r.width)/r.width,
      sy:(typeof stageH!=='undefined'&&Number(stageH)>0?Number(stageH):r.height)/r.height,moved:false};
    try{moveDrag.btn.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
    /* v231: suppress v80 handle repairs during this text move. */
    window.__evTextDragActive=true;
  }
  /* v231: one-element paint plus one selection-box update per animation frame. */
  function paintMoveDrag(d,x,y,finishOnly){
    if(!d)return;
    const dx=(x-d.startX)*d.sx,dy=(y-d.startY)*d.sy;
    if(!d.moved&&Math.hypot(dx,dy)<1.5)return;d.moved=true;
    d.ov.transform.offsetX=d.ox+dx;d.ov.transform.offsetY=d.oy+dy;
    if(!finishOnly){
      if(!window.ethvelleTextDragPaintFast(d.ov))try{if(typeof renderOverlayMedia==='function')renderOverlayMedia(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():0);}catch(_e){}
      try{if(typeof updateSelBox==='function')updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}
    }
  }
  let moveDragRaf=0;
  function onMoveDrag(e){
    const d=moveDrag;if(!d||d.id!==e.pointerId)return;stop(e);
    d.lastX=e.clientX;d.lastY=e.clientY;
    if(moveDragRaf)return;
    moveDragRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{moveDragRaf=0;if(moveDrag===d)paintMoveDrag(d,d.lastX,d.lastY);});
  }
  function finishMove(e,cancelled){
    const d=moveDrag;if(!d||d.id!==e.pointerId)return;stop(e);
    if(moveDragRaf)cancelAnimationFrame(moveDragRaf);moveDragRaf=0;
    if(Number.isFinite(e.clientX)&&Number.isFinite(e.clientY))paintMoveDrag(d,e.clientX,e.clientY,true);
    moveDrag=null;window.__evTextDragActive=false; /* v231: release drag suppression. */
    try{d.btn.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
    if(cancelled&&d.moved){d.ov.transform.offsetX=d.ox;d.ov.transform.offsetY=d.oy;}
    if(d.moved){try{if(typeof renderOverlayMedia==='function')renderOverlayMedia(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():0);}catch(_e){}try{if(typeof updateSelBox==='function')updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}if(!cancelled){try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}catch(_e){}}}
    /* v231: updateSelBox's existing wrapper performs the single corner layout. */
  }
  window.addEventListener('pointerdown',onMoveDown,{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('pointermove',onMoveDrag,{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>finishMove(e,false),{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('pointercancel',e=>finishMove(e,true),{capture:true,passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target?.closest?.('#sbTextMove'))stop(e);},{capture:true,passive:false});

  // Functional eyedropper with a zoomed preview and sampled-color rim. This keeps the actual
  // color application API used by the rest of Ethvelle, rather than replacing the picker itself.
  function rgbHex(value){
    const m=String(value||'').match(/rgba?\(\s*([\d.]+)\s*[, ]\s*([\d.]+)\s*[, ]\s*([\d.]+)(?:\s*[,/]\s*([\d.]+%?))?\s*\)/i);
    if(!m)return null;let alpha=m[4]===undefined?1:parseFloat(m[4]);if(String(m[4]||'').includes('%'))alpha/=100;if(alpha<=.025)return null;
    return '#'+m.slice(1,4).map(v=>Math.round(clamp(Number(v),0,255)).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
  }
  function pixelCanvas(el,x,y){
    try{const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();if(!r.width||!r.height||!el.width||!el.height)return null;
      const px=clamp(Math.floor((x-r.left)*el.width/r.width),0,el.width-1),py=clamp(Math.floor((y-r.top)*el.height/r.height),0,el.height-1);
      const d=el.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true})?.getImageData(px,py,1,1).data;if(!d||d[3]<8)return null;
      return '#'+[d[0],d[1],d[2]].map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function pixelMedia(el,x,y){
    try{const video=el.tagName==='VIDEO',nw=video?(el.videoWidth||0):(el.naturalWidth||0),nh=video?(el.videoHeight||0):(el.naturalHeight||0);if(!nw||!nh||(video&&el.readyState<2))return null;
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();if(!r.width||!r.height)return null;let sx=(x-r.left)/r.width*nw,sy=(y-r.top)/r.height*nh;
      const fit=getComputedStyle(el).objectFit||'fill';if(fit==='cover'||fit==='contain'){const scale=fit==='cover'?Math.max(r.width/nw,r.height/nh):Math.min(r.width/nw,r.height/nh);sx=(x-r.left-(r.width-nw*scale)/2)/scale;sy=(y-r.top-(r.height-nh*scale)/2)/scale;}
      sx=clamp(Math.floor(sx),0,nw-1);sy=clamp(Math.floor(sy),0,nh-1);const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=1;const cx=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});cx.drawImage(el,sx,sy,1,1,0,0,1,1);const d=cx.getImageData(0,0,1,1).data;if(d[3]<8)return null;
      return '#'+[d[0],d[1],d[2]].map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function sampleAt(stage,x,y){
    const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();if(x<r.left||x>r.right||y<r.top||y>r.bottom)return null;
    let stack=[];try{stack=document.elementsFromPoint(x,y).filter(el=>el===stage||stage.contains(el));}catch(_e){}
    for(const el of stack){
      if(el.tagName==='CANVAS'){const c=pixelCanvas(el,x,y);if(c)return c;}
      if(el.tagName==='IMG'||el.tagName==='VIDEO'){const c=pixelMedia(el,x,y);if(c)return c;}
      const cs=getComputedStyle(el);
      if(el.classList?.contains('ethvelle-overlay-text-content')){const t=rgbHex(cs.webkitTextFillColor)||rgbHex(cs.color);if(t)return t;}
      const fill=rgbHex(cs.fill);if(fill&&el.namespaceURI==='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg')return fill;
      const bg=rgbHex(cs.backgroundColor);if(bg&&Number(cs.opacity||1)>.025)return bg;
    }
    return rgbHex(getComputedStyle(stage).backgroundColor)||'#777777';
  }
  function cloneStage(stage,viewport){
    const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect(),scene=stage.cloneNode(true),imp=(el,k,v)=>el.style.setProperty(k,v,'important');
    scene.classList.add('evpa-loupe-scene');
    [['position','absolute'],['left','0px'],['top','0px'],['width',r.width+'px'],['height',r.height+'px'],['max-width','none'],['max-height','none'],['margin','0'],['transform-origin','0 0'],['overflow','hidden'],['pointer-events','none']].forEach(([k,v])=>imp(scene,k,v));
    const srcCs=stage.querySelectorAll('canvas'),dstCs=scene.querySelectorAll('canvas');
    for(let i=0;i<Math.min(srcCs.length,dstCs.length);i++){try{dstCs[i].width=srcCs[i].width;dstCs[i].height=srcCs[i].height;dstCs[i].getContext('2d')?.drawImage(srcCs[i],0,0,dstCs[i].width,dstCs[i].height);}catch(_e){}}
    const srcVs=stage.querySelectorAll('video'),dstVs=scene.querySelectorAll('video');
    for(let i=0;i<Math.min(srcVs.length,dstVs.length);i++){try{const v=srcVs[i],dst=dstVs[i];if(!v.videoWidth||!v.videoHeight||v.readyState<2)continue;const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=v.videoWidth;c.height=v.videoHeight;const cs=getComputedStyle(v);['position','left','top','right','bottom','width','height','object-fit','object-position','transform','transform-origin','opacity','z-index','border-radius','display'].forEach(k=>{const val=cs.getPropertyValue(k);if(val)c.style.setProperty(k,val,'important');});c.getContext('2d')?.drawImage(v,0,0,c.width,c.height);dst.replaceWith(c);}catch(_e){}}
    viewport.appendChild(scene);return scene;
  }
  function pickerColorKey(){
    const supplied=typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget():'';
    const tab=$('evpaTabs')?.querySelector('.evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab||'color';
    const fallback=tab==='stroke'?'strokeColor':tab==='shadow'?'shadowColor':(['highlight','background'].includes(tab)?'textBackgroundColor':'textColor');
    return ['textColor','strokeColor','shadowColor','textBackgroundColor'].includes(supplied)?supplied:fallback;
  }
  window.ethvellePicsartSampleColor=function(){
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Canvas is not available');return;}
    if(window.__ethvelleV17Sampling)return;window.__ethvelleV17Sampling=true;
    const key=pickerColorKey(),picker=$('ethvelleColorPicker'),pickerWasOpen=!!picker?.classList.contains('active');
    const roots=[$('ethvelleTextComposer'),picker,$('ethvelleTextStylePanel'),$('ethvellePicsartTextDock')].filter(Boolean);
    const saved=roots.map(el=>({el,visibility:el.style.visibility}));roots.forEach(el=>el.style.visibility='hidden');refreshShields();
    let loupe=$('evpaSamplerLoupe');if(!loupe){loupe=document.createElement('div');loupe.id='evpaSamplerLoupe';document.body.appendChild(loupe);}
    loupe.innerHTML='<div class="evpa-loupe-viewport"><div class="evpa-loupe-crosshair"></div></div>';
    const viewport=loupe.querySelector('.evpa-loupe-viewport');let scene=null;try{scene=cloneStage(stage,viewport);}catch(_e){}
    loupe.style.setProperty('--evpa-sample-color','#777777');loupe.style.display='block';
    let pointer=null,lastColor='#777777';const zoom=2.35;let closed=false;
    const inside=(x,y)=>{const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();return x>=r.left&&x<=r.right&&y>=r.top&&y<=r.bottom;};
    const updateLens=(x,y)=>{
      const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();const width=loupe.getBoundingClientRect().width||150,half=width/2;
      const lx=clamp(x,half+3,Math.max(half+3,innerWidth-half-3)),ly=clamp(y,half+3,Math.max(half+3,innerHeight-half-3));
      loupe.style.left=lx+'px';loupe.style.top=ly+'px';
      const c=sampleAt(stage,x,y);if(c){lastColor=c;loupe.style.setProperty('--evpa-sample-color',c);}
      if(scene){const vw=Math.max(30,viewport.clientWidth),vh=Math.max(30,viewport.clientHeight);scene.style.setProperty('left',(vw/2-(x-r.left)*zoom)+'px','important');scene.style.setProperty('top',(vh/2-(y-r.top)*zoom)+'px','important');scene.style.setProperty('transform','scale('+zoom+')','important');}
      refreshShields();return c;
    };
    const stopSample=e=>{e.preventDefault?.();e.stopPropagation?.();e.stopImmediatePropagation?.();};
    const cleanup=()=>{
      if(closed)return;closed=true;
      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown',begin,true);window.removeEventListener('pointermove',move,true);window.removeEventListener('pointerup',end,true);window.removeEventListener('pointercancel',cancel,true);document.removeEventListener('keydown',escapeCancel,true);
      loupe.style.display='none';viewport.replaceChildren();saved.forEach(o=>o.el.style.visibility=o.visibility);window.__ethvelleV17Sampling=false;refreshShields();
    };
    const begin=e=>{if(pointer!==null||!inside(e.clientX,e.clientY))return;pointer=e.pointerId;stopSample(e);try{e.target?.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}updateLens(e.clientX,e.clientY);};
    const move=e=>{if(pointer===null||e.pointerId!==pointer)return;stopSample(e);updateLens(e.clientX,e.clientY);};
    const end=e=>{
      if(pointer===null||e.pointerId!==pointer)return;stopSample(e);const x=e.clientX,y=e.clientY;const sampled=inside(x,y)?(sampleAt(stage,x,y)||lastColor):null;cleanup();
      if(sampled&&typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColor==='function'){window.ethvellePicsartSetColor(key,sampled);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Canvas color applied',{duration:1100});}
      else if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Pick a visible color inside the canvas',{duration:1500});
      if(sampled&&pickerWasOpen&&typeof window.openEthvelleColorPicker==='function')requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.openEthvelleColorPicker(key));
    };
    const cancel=()=>cleanup(),escapeCancel=e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')cleanup();};
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown',begin,{capture:true,passive:false});window.addEventListener('pointermove',move,{capture:true,passive:false});window.addEventListener('pointerup',end,{capture:true,passive:false});window.addEventListener('pointercancel',cancel,true);document.addEventListener('keydown',escapeCancel,true);
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Drag across the canvas and release to pick its color',{duration:1400});
  };
  function finalBoot(){refreshShields();layoutCorners();}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',finalBoot,{once:true});else finalBoot();
})();
</script>


<!-- Ethvelle text-layer workflow update v17: normal layer tools + dedicated Text edit action -->
<style id="ethvelle-text-layer-workflow-v17-css">
  /* The text-creation composer stays focused on writing; effects live in the dedicated editor. */

  /* Canvas sampling / eyedropper has been removed from all text color controls. */
  .evpa-eye,
  #ethvelleColorPicker .evpa-picker-actions button[onclick*="SampleColor"],
  #ethvelleColorPicker .evpa-picker-actions button[onclick*="sampleColor"],
  #ethvelleColorPicker .evpa-picker-actions button[aria-label*="canvas" i],
  #ethvelleColorPicker .evpa-picker-actions button[title*="canvas" i],
  #ethvelleColorPicker .evpa-picker-actions button[aria-label*="sample" i],
  #ethvelleColorPicker .evpa-picker-actions button[title*="sample" i],
  #ethvelleColorPicker .evcp-actions .evcp-secondary,
  #ethvelleColorPicker button[onclick*="StageColorSample"],
  #evpaSamplerLoupe{display:none!important;}
  /* Keep the selection handles used for resize/move, but move Text edit into the bottom toolbar. */

<style id="ethvelle-v231-text-dock-safety-css">
  /* v231: keep the main dock hidden unless an explicit editor action owns it; composer is exempt. */
  body:not(.evpa-text-editor-open) #ethvellePicsartTextDock:not(.evpa-in-composer){display:none!important;}

<script id="ethvelle-text-layer-workflow-v17-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const TEXT_TOOL_IDS=['clipTextEditTool','clipTextFontTool','clipTextStrokeTool','clipTextShadowTool','clipTextColorTool','clipTextBackgroundTool'];
  let frameDismissedAfterTextEditClose=false;
  let textEditorExplicitlyOpened=false;
  /* v231: one owner keeps the explicit-open flag and body safety class in sync. */
  window.__evSetTextEditorOpen=function(v){window.__evTextEditorOpen=!!v;document.body.classList.toggle('evpa-text-editor-open',!!v);};
  window.__evSetTextEditorOpen(false);
  let syncing=false;

  function selectedTextOverlay(){
    try{return Array.isArray(window.overlays||overlays)?(window.overlays||overlays).find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(window.selectedClip??selectedClip)&&o.type==='text'):null;}
    catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function makeTextEditButton(){
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip');if(!row)return null;
    let btn=$('clipTextEditTool');
    if(!btn){
      btn=document.createElement('div');btn.id='clipTextEditTool';btn.className='tool-item ev-text-tool';btn.setAttribute('role','button');btn.tabIndex=0;
      row.insertBefore(btn,row.firstChild);
    }
    if(btn.dataset.evTextEditV17!=='1'){
      btn.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 20h9"/><path d="M16.5 3.5a2.1 2.1 0 0 1 3 3L8 18l-4 1 1-4Z"/></svg><span>Text edit</span>';
      btn.setAttribute('aria-label','Text edit');btn.title='Text edit';
      btn.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();const clip=selectedTextOverlay();if(!clip)return;if(typeof window.selectClipForChrome==='function')window.selectClipForChrome(clip.id);frameDismissedAfterTextEditClose=false;if(typeof window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel==='function')window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel('font');};
      btn.onkeydown=function(e){if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();btn.click();}};
      btn.dataset.evTextEditV17='1';
    }
    return btn;
  }
  function setDisplay(id,value){const el=$(id);if(el)el.style.display=value;}
  function refreshToolbar(){
    if(syncing)return;syncing=true;
    try{
      makeTextEditButton();
      const clip=selectedTextOverlay();
      const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
      const composerActive=!!($('ethvelleTextComposer')&&$('ethvelleTextComposer').classList.contains('active'));
      const editing=!!(textEditorExplicitlyOpened&&dock&&dock.style.display!=='none'&&!dock.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer'));
      TEXT_TOOL_IDS.forEach(id=>setDisplay(id,'none'));
      if(clip){
        // Text styling opens only through the explicit Text edit action, never merely by selection.
        if(!textEditorExplicitlyOpened&&!composerActive&&dock){dock.style.display='none';dock.classList.remove('evpa-in-composer');}
        // Match the regular overlay-layer toolbar; keep only the dedicated text-edit button
        // from the text-specific controls. These options operate on the selected layer itself.
        const show=['clipMediaAccessTool','clipSplitTool','clipTrimTool','clipAddMediaTool','clipTransformTool','clipForwardTool','clipBackwardTool','clipDuplicateTool'];
        show.forEach(id=>{const el=$(id);if(el)el.style.display='flex';});
        // Text layers are visual overlays, not audio/video playback clips or character rigs.
        ['clipAnimTool','clipSpeedTool','clipVolumeTool','clipChromaTool','clipExtractAudioTool','clipDeleteCharacterTool'].forEach(id=>setDisplay(id,'none'));
        const editBtn=$('clipTextEditTool');if(editBtn)editBtn.style.display=editing?'none':'flex';
        const row=$('bottomToolsClip');if(row){row.style.visibility=(editing||composerActive)?'hidden':'';row.style.pointerEvents=(editing||composerActive)?'none':'';}
      }
    }catch(err){console.warn('Text-layer toolbar refresh failed',err);}
    finally{syncing=false;}
  }

  // Wrap the current (multi-version) toolbar synchronizer only once, then restore normal
  // overlay controls for text clips after legacy text-tool code has run.
  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__ethvelleV17TextToolbar){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);refreshToolbar();return r;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV17TextToolbar=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }

  // Selecting a text clip brings back its normal white transform frame. Closing the text editor
  // can dismiss that frame without deselecting the clip, so the usual bottom layer options remain.
  const oldUpdate=window.updateSelBox;
  if(typeof oldUpdate==='function'&&!oldUpdate.__ethvelleV17Frame){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldUpdate.apply(this,arguments);if(frameDismissedAfterTextEditClose&&selectedTextOverlay()){const layer=$('selBoxLayer');if(layer)layer.style.display='none';}return r;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV17Frame=true;window.updateSelBox=wrapped;
  }
  const oldSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect==='function'&&!oldSelect.__ethvelleV17Frame){
    const wrapped=function(which){frameDismissedAfterTextEditClose=false;textEditorExplicitlyOpened=false;/* v231: selection is not an editor-open action. */window.__evSetTextEditorOpen(false);const r=oldSelect.apply(this,arguments);refreshToolbar();return r;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV17Frame=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }

  function closeTextEditKeepLayerSelected(){
    textEditorExplicitlyOpened=false;/* v231: keep the safety class aligned with Close. */window.__evSetTextEditorOpen(false);
    try{
      const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer');
      if(composer&&composer.classList.contains('active')&&typeof window.closeEthvelleTextComposer==='function')window.closeEthvelleTextComposer(false);
    }catch(_e){}
    $('ethvelleColorPicker')?.classList.remove('active');
    $('ethvelleTextStylePanel')?.classList.remove('active','evts-compose-subpanel');
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(dock){dock.classList.remove('evpa-in-composer');dock.style.display='none';}
    const root=$('ethvelleTextComposer');if(root){root.classList.remove('active');root.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
    $('screen-editor')?.classList.remove('ev-text-backdrop-blur');
    document.body.classList.remove('evtc-is-open');
    frameDismissedAfterTextEditClose=!!selectedTextOverlay();
    if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')window.syncBottomToolsMode();
    // Legacy sync wrappers try to reopen the text dock whenever a text clip is selected.
    // Keep it closed after the user explicitly presses Close, while preserving selection.
    if(dock)dock.style.display='none';
    if(frameDismissedAfterTextEditClose){const layer=$('selBoxLayer');if(layer)layer.style.display='none';}
    refreshToolbar();
  }
  window.ethvelleCloseTextEditingV17=closeTextEditKeepLayerSelected;
  // Capture before older close-tab listeners. The selected clip remains active, so the normal
  // layer toolbar (including Text edit) returns instead of falling back to the default toolbar.
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    const close=e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-close-tab');
    if(!close)return;e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation();closeTextEditKeepLayerSelected();
  },true);

  // Remove canvas eyedropper behavior from old buttons and any stale callers.
  window.ethvellePicsartSampleColor=function(){if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Canvas color picking is disabled',{duration:1200});};
  window.ethvelleStartStageColorSample=function(){if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Canvas color picking is disabled',{duration:1200});};
  window.ethvellePicsartSetColorTarget=window.ethvellePicsartSetColorTarget||function(){};
  function removeSamplerButtons(){
    /* The old canvas sampler is disabled, but the Font palette still has a visible
       eyedropper control. Preserve only that dedicated Font-row button; remove legacy
       sampler buttons from every other picker/composer location as before. */
    document.querySelectorAll('.evpa-eye').forEach(el=>{
      if(!el.closest('#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-font-color-tools'))el.remove();
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('#ethvelleColorPicker .evcp-actions .evcp-secondary,#ethvelleColorPicker .evpa-picker-actions button[onclick*="SampleColor"],#ethvelleColorPicker .evpa-picker-actions button[onclick*="sampleColor"],#ethvelleColorPicker button[onclick*="StageColorSample"]').forEach(el=>el.remove());
    const loupe=$('evpaSamplerLoupe');if(loupe)loupe.remove();
  }
  const oldOpenTextStyle=window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel;
  if(typeof oldOpenTextStyle==='function'&&!oldOpenTextStyle.__ethvelleV17Explicit){
    const wrapped=function(){textEditorExplicitlyOpened=true;/* v231: set open state before legacy dock checks run. */window.__evSetTextEditorOpen(true);const r=oldOpenTextStyle.apply(this,arguments);refreshToolbar();return r;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV17Explicit=true;window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel=wrapped;
  }

  function boot(){
    makeTextEditButton();refreshToolbar();removeSamplerButtons();
    const mo=new MutationObserver(()=>{makeTextEditButton();refreshToolbar();removeSamplerButtons();});
    if(document.body)mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
    document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      if(e.target?.closest?.('#timelineInner,#overlayRows,.ethvelle-overlay-text-content'))setTimeout(refreshToolbar,0);
    },true);
    window.addEventListener('resize',refreshToolbar,{passive:true});
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>

  #bottomToolsClip #clipTextEditTool{background:transparent!important;border:0!important;box-shadow:none!important;border-radius:8px!important;color:#E5E9F8!important;}
