  const BENGALI_FONT_NAMES=new Set(['Hind Siliguri','Noto Sans Bengali','Noto Serif Bengali','Anek Bangla','Alkatra','Atma','Baloo Da 2','Galada','Google Sans','Mina','Tiro Bangla','Solaiman Lipi','Kalpurush','Siyam Rupali','Lohit Bengali','Bornomala','Bornomala Vintage','Ekushey Lal Sabuj','Adorsho Lipi','Charukola','Ekushey Mukto','Bensen','Bensen Handwriting','Ekushey Saraswatii','Ekushey Azad','Hoogli','Bornoporichay','Aikya','Kalaa','UN Bangla','Tuli']);
  let gradientPickState=null, observerQueued=false;
  function getTarget(){try{return typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;}catch(_){return null;}}
  function persist(){try{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();}catch(_){}try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}catch(_){try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState();}catch(__){}}}
  function glyphIcon(name){
    const paths={left:'<path d="M4 6h16M4 10h10M4 14h16M4 18h10"/>',center:'<path d="M4 6h16M7 10h10M4 14h16M7 18h10"/>',right:'<path d="M4 6h16M10 10h10M4 14h16M10 18h10"/>'};
    return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">'+(paths[name]||paths.left)+'</svg>';
  }
  function ensureFormatTab(){
    const tabs=$('evpaTabs');if(!tabs)return;
    let b=tabs.querySelector('[data-tab="format"]');
    if(!b){b=document.createElement('button');b.className='evpa-tab';b.type='button';b.dataset.tab='format';b.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 5h14M12 5v14M8 19h8"/></svg><span>Format</span>';b.addEventListener('click',()=>window.ethvellePicsartSetTool('format'));const bend=tabs.querySelector('[data-tab="bend"]');if(bend&&bend.nextSibling)tabs.insertBefore(b,bend.nextSibling);else tabs.appendChild(b);}
    const bg=tabs.querySelector('[data-tab="background"]');if(bg)bg.querySelector('span').textContent='BG';
    tabs.querySelectorAll('.evpa-tab').forEach(x=>x.setAttribute('aria-label',x.querySelector('span')?.textContent||''));
  }
  function currentFontName(){const t=getTarget();return String((t&&t.fontFamily)||'Inter');}
  function fontLinkId(name){return 'ethvelle-font-'+String(name).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-');}
  /* Resolves when the family's stylesheet + glyph data are ready (or after a timeout), so the text is
     repainted with the real font instead of the fallback. A just-added <link> has no @font-face yet,
     so wait for it first; document.fonts.load() then fetches the glyphs for the actual text. */
  function waitForFont(name,timeoutMs){
    return new Promise(resolve=>{
      let finished=false;
      const timer=setTimeout(()=>{if(!finished){finished=true;resolve();}},timeoutMs||2500);
      const done=()=>{if(finished)return;finished=true;clearTimeout(timer);resolve();};
      try{if(typeof window.ensureAdminFontLoaded==='function')window.ensureAdminFontLoaded(name);}catch(_){}
      if(!document.fonts||typeof document.fonts.load!=='function'){done();return;}
      const link=document.getElementById(fontLinkId(name));
      const stylesheetReady=new Promise(r=>{
        if(!link||link.tagName!=='LINK'||link.sheet){r();return;}
        link.addEventListener('load',()=>r(),{once:true});link.addEventListener('error',()=>r(),{once:true});
      });
      const t=getTarget(),stage=$('evtcStageText');
      const sample=String((t&&t.textContent)||(stage&&stage.value)||'Aa').slice(0,60)||'Aa';
      const query='16px "'+String(name).replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/"/g,'\\"')+'"';
      stylesheetReady.then(()=>document.fonts.load(query,sample)).then(done,done);
    });
  }
  let fontPickSeq=0;
  function syncFontCards(gallery,name){
    gallery.querySelectorAll('.evpa-font-card').forEach(c=>c.classList.toggle('active',c.dataset.font===name));
  }
  function applyFont(gallery,name){
    const seq=++fontPickSeq;
    /* 1) data + immediate repaint (no panel rebuild, see styleTarget) */
    try{window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('fontFamily',name);}catch(_){}
    /* 2) once the glyphs exist, repaint again and commit one undo step */
    waitForFont(name).then(()=>{
      if(seq!==fontPickSeq)return;
      const t=getTarget();if(!t||String(t.fontFamily)!==name)return;
      try{if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartRepaintText==='function')window.ethvellePicsartRepaintText();}catch(_){}
      const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer');
      if(!(composer&&composer.classList.contains('active'))){try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState();}catch(_){}}
      if(gallery.isConnected)syncFontCards(gallery,name);
    });
  }
  function makeFontGallery(controls){
    if(!controls||controls.querySelector('.evpa-font-gallery'))return;
    if(!controls.querySelector('[data-evpa-panel="font"]'))return;
    const current=currentFontName();
    const gallery=document.createElement('div');gallery.className='evpa-font-gallery';gallery.setAttribute('aria-label','Choose a font');
    gallery.innerHTML=FONT_CHOICES.map(([font,label])=>{
      const safeFont=font.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
      const safeLabel=label.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
      const sample=BENGALI_FONT_NAMES.has(font)?'বাংলা':'Aa';
      return '<button type="button" class="evpa-font-card '+(font===current?'active':'')+'" data-font="'+safeFont+'" aria-label="'+safeLabel+'"><span class="evpa-font-sample" data-font-preview="'+safeFont+'" style="font-family:Inter,sans-serif">'+sample+'</span><span class="evpa-font-name">'+safeLabel+'</span></button>';
    }).join('');
    const previewNodes=gallery.querySelectorAll('.evpa-font-sample[data-font-preview]');
    const loadPreview=node=>{
      const family=node.getAttribute('data-font-preview')||'Inter';
      try{if(typeof window.ensureAdminFontLoaded==='function')window.ensureAdminFontLoaded(family);}catch(_){}
      node.style.fontFamily='"'+family.replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/"/g,'\\"')+'",sans-serif';
    };
    if(typeof IntersectionObserver==='function'){
      const fontPreviewObserver=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(entry=>{
        if(!entry.isIntersecting)return;
        loadPreview(entry.target);fontPreviewObserver.unobserve(entry.target);
      }),{root:gallery,rootMargin:'0px 260px 0px 260px'});
      previewNodes.forEach(node=>fontPreviewObserver.observe(node));
    }else previewNodes.forEach(loadPreview);
    gallery.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      const card=e.target.closest('.evpa-font-card');if(!card||!gallery.contains(card))return;
      const val=card.dataset.font;if(!val||!getTarget())return;
      syncFontCards(gallery,val);          /* instant, in-place highlight */
      applyFont(gallery,val);
    });
    const heading=controls.querySelector('.evpa-control-heading');
    if(heading)heading.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',gallery);else controls.insertBefore(gallery,controls.firstChild);
  }
  window.ethvelleEnsureFontGallery=makeFontGallery;
  function formatMarkup(){
    const t=getTarget()||{};
    const active=(key,yes)=>yes?' active':'';
    const bold=Number(t.fontWeight||400)>=700, italic=String(t.fontStyle||'normal')==='italic';
    const underline=!!t.textUnderline,strike=!!t.textStrikethrough,tc=String(t.textTransform||'none');
    const align=String(t.textAlign||'center');
    return '<div class="evpa-format-panel">'+
      '<div class="evpa-format-row" role="group" aria-label="Text style">'+
      '<button type="button" class="evpa-format-btn'+active('bold',bold)+'" aria-label="Bold" title="Bold" onclick="ethvelleFormatAction(\'bold\')"><span class="evpa-format-glyph" style="font-weight:800">B</span></button><span class="evpa-format-divider"></span>'+
      '<button type="button" class="evpa-format-btn'+active('italic',italic)+'" aria-label="Italic" title="Italic" onclick="ethvelleFormatAction(\'italic\')"><span class="evpa-format-glyph" style="font-style:italic">I</span></button><span class="evpa-format-divider"></span>'+
      '<button type="button" class="evpa-format-btn'+active('underline',underline)+'" aria-label="Underline" title="Underline" onclick="ethvelleFormatAction(\'underline\')"><span class="evpa-format-glyph" style="text-decoration:underline">U</span></button><span class="evpa-format-divider"></span>'+
      '<button type="button" class="evpa-format-btn'+active('strike',strike)+'" aria-label="Strikethrough" title="Strikethrough" onclick="ethvelleFormatAction(\'strike\')"><span class="evpa-format-glyph" style="text-decoration:line-through">S</span></button></div>'+
      '<div class="evpa-format-row" role="group" aria-label="Case and alignment">'+
      '<button type="button" class="evpa-format-btn'+active('upper',tc==='uppercase')+'" aria-label="Uppercase" title="UPPERCASE" onclick="ethvelleFormatSetCase(\'uppercase\')"><span style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800">AA</span></button>'+
      '<button type="button" class="evpa-format-btn'+active('title',tc==='capitalize')+'" aria-label="Capitalize words" title="Capitalize words" onclick="ethvelleFormatSetCase(\'capitalize\')"><span style="font-size:18px">Aa</span></button>'+
      '<button type="button" class="evpa-format-btn'+active('lower',tc==='lowercase')+'" aria-label="Lowercase" title="lowercase" onclick="ethvelleFormatSetCase(\'lowercase\')"><span style="font-size:18px">aa</span></button><span class="evpa-format-divider"></span>'+
      '<button type="button" class="evpa-format-btn'+active('left',align==='left')+'" aria-label="Align left" title="Align left" onclick="ethvelleFormatSetAlign(\'left\')">'+glyphIcon('left')+'</button>'+
      '<button type="button" class="evpa-format-btn'+active('center',align==='center'||!align)+'" aria-label="Align center" title="Align center" onclick="ethvelleFormatSetAlign(\'center\')">'+glyphIcon('center')+'</button>'+
      '<button type="button" class="evpa-format-btn'+active('right',align==='right')+'" aria-label="Align right" title="Align right" onclick="ethvelleFormatSetAlign(\'right\')">'+glyphIcon('right')+'</button></div></div>';
  }
  window.ethvelleFormatAction=function(action){
    const t=getTarget();if(!t)return;
    if(action==='bold')window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('fontWeight',Number(t.fontWeight||400)>=700?500:800);
    if(action==='italic')window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('fontStyle',t.fontStyle==='italic'?'normal':'italic');
    if(action==='underline')window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('textUnderline',!t.textUnderline);
    if(action==='strike')window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('textStrikethrough',!t.textStrikethrough);
    setTimeout(()=>refreshLayout(true),0);
  };
  window.ethvelleFormatSetCase=function(value){const t=getTarget();if(!t)return;t.textTransform=['uppercase','lowercase','capitalize'].includes(value)?value:'none';applyTextTransforms();persist();refreshLayout(true);};
  window.ethvelleFormatSetAlign=function(value){if(!['left','center','right'].includes(value))return;window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('textAlign',value);setTimeout(()=>refreshLayout(true),0);};
  function applyTextTransforms(){
    let arr=[];try{arr=typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:(window.overlays||[]);}catch(_){arr=window.overlays||[];}
    const layer=$('overlayMediaLayer');
    (arr||[]).filter(o=>o&&o.type==='text').forEach(o=>{
      let host=null;try{if(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)host=overlayMediaEls[o.id]||null;}catch(_){}
      if(!host&&layer)host=Array.from(layer.children).find(n=>String(n.dataset.overlayId||n.dataset.ovId||'')===String(o.id));
      const node=host?.querySelector?.('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content');if(node)node.style.textTransform=o.textTransform||'none';
    });
    const composer=$('evtcStageText'),draft=getTarget();if(composer&&draft)composer.style.textTransform=draft.textTransform||'none';
  }
  function refreshLayout(force){
    ensureFormatTab();const controls=$('evpaControls');if(!controls)return;
    const active=$('evpaTabs')?.querySelector('.evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab;
    if(active==='format'){
      if(force||!controls.querySelector('.evpa-format-panel'))controls.innerHTML=formatMarkup();
      return;
    }
    if(active==='font'){
      if(!controls.querySelector('.evpa-font-gallery'))makeFontGallery(controls);
      controls.querySelectorAll('.evpa-choice-row,.evpa-wide-btn').forEach(el=>el.style.display='none');
    }
    if(active==='texture'){
      const add=controls.querySelector('.evpa-effect-add');
      if(add){add.setAttribute('aria-label','Upload a custom texture image');add.title='Upload an image texture';add.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();window.openEthvelleTextureImageUpload();};}
      makeImageTextureChip();
    }
    const editor=$('evpaCustomEffectEditor');if(editor&&editor.classList.contains('active'))decorateGradientColorInputs(editor);
    if(active==='font'||active==='texture')applyTextTransforms();
  }
  window.ethvellePicsartSetTool=(function(original){return function(tool){const out=original?original.apply(this,arguments):undefined;/* The base tool renderer already handles Shadow/Bend and other ordinary tabs. Only tabs with separately-built UI need the extra pass. */if(tool==='format'||tool==='font'||tool==='texture')refreshLayout(true);return out;};})(window.ethvellePicsartSetTool);
  function scheduleRefresh(){if(observerQueued)return;observerQueued=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{observerQueued=false;refreshLayout(false);});}
  function buildImageTextureInput(){
    if($('ethvelleTextureImageInput'))return $('ethvelleTextureImageInput');
    const input=document.createElement('input');input.type='file';input.id='ethvelleTextureImageInput';input.accept='image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp,image/gif,image/avif';input.hidden=true;document.body.appendChild(input);
    input.addEventListener('change',()=>{const file=input.files&&input.files[0];input.value='';if(!file)return;if(!/^image\//.test(file.type)){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Choose an image file');return;}if(file.size>20*1024*1024){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Image is too large. Choose an image under 20 MB.');return;}
      const url=URL.createObjectURL(file),img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{try{const max=512,scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth||img.width,img.naturalHeight||img.height));const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=Math.max(1,Math.round((img.naturalWidth||img.width)*scale));cv.height=Math.max(1,Math.round((img.naturalHeight||img.height)*scale));const cx=cv.getContext('2d');cx.drawImage(img,0,0,cv.width,cv.height);let data=cv.toDataURL('image/webp',.86);if(!data||data==='data:,')data=cv.toDataURL('image/png');const css='url("'+data+'")';
          if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetTexture==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetTexture(css,'100% 100%');
          try{const key='ethvelleCustomTextTexturesV1',list=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');list.unshift({name:String(file.name||'Image texture').replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'').slice(0,18)||'Image texture',css,size:'100% 100%'});localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(list.slice(0,5)));}catch(_){/* project state still retains the currently applied image texture */}
          if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Image texture applied to text',{icon:typeof toastIcons!=='undefined'?toastIcons.check:undefined,duration:1600});
        }catch(err){console.error('Text image texture failed',err);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not create a texture from this image');}finally{URL.revokeObjectURL(url);}};img.onerror=()=>{URL.revokeObjectURL(url);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not open that image');};img.src=url;
    });return input;
  }
  window.openEthvelleTextureImageUpload=function(){buildImageTextureInput().click();};
  function makeImageTextureChip(){
    const host=$('evpaControls');if(!host||!host.querySelector('.evpa-gradient-presets'))return;
    const presets=host.querySelector('.evpa-gradient-presets');if(presets.querySelector('[data-evpa-image-upload]'))return;
    const b=presets.querySelector('.evpa-effect-add');if(b){b.dataset.evpaImageUpload='1';b.innerHTML='+';b.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();window.openEthvelleTextureImageUpload();};}
    let saved=[];try{saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ethvelleCustomTextTexturesV1')||'[]').slice(0,5);}catch(_){}
    const customOffset=presets.querySelectorAll('.evpa-custom-texture-chip').length;
    saved.forEach((item,i)=>{if(presets.querySelector('[data-image-texture-index="'+i+'"]'))return;const chip=document.createElement('button');chip.type='button';chip.className='evpa-gradient-chip evpa-image-texture-chip';chip.dataset.imageTextureIndex=String(i);chip.dataset.evpaEffect='texture';chip.dataset.evpaFill=encodeURIComponent(item.css||'');chip.title='Custom texture '+(customOffset+i+1);chip.textContent='Custom '+(customOffset+i+1);chip.style.backgroundImage=item.css||'none';chip.style.backgroundSize=item.size||'100% 100%';chip.onclick=()=>window.ethvellePicsartSetTexture(item.css,item.size);presets.appendChild(chip);});
  }
  function targetFromInput(input){return input?.closest('.evce-stop')?.querySelector('[data-stop-color]')||input;}
  window.openEthvelleGradientStopColor=function(button){
    window.__ETHVELLE_PICKER_FOR_GRADIENT_STOP=true;
    const input=button?.parentElement?.querySelector('[data-stop-color]')||button?.parentElement?.querySelector('input[type="color"]');
    if(!input||typeof window.openEthvelleColorPicker!=='function')return;
    const t=getTarget();if(!t)return;
    gradientPickState={input,oldTextColor:t.textColor||'#FFFFFF',target:t,oldTextOpacity:t.textOpacity};
    window.openEthvelleColorPicker('textColor');
  };
  function finishGradientColorPick(){
    if(!gradientPickState)return;const st=gradientPickState;gradientPickState=null;window.__ETHVELLE_PICKER_FOR_GRADIENT_STOP=false;
    const chosen=String(st.target?.textColor||'#FFFFFF');
    if(st.target){st.target.textColor=st.oldTextColor;if(st.oldTextOpacity!==undefined)st.target.textOpacity=st.oldTextOpacity;else delete st.target.textOpacity;}
    if(st.input&&st.input.isConnected){st.input.value=/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(chosen)?chosen:'#FFFFFF';st.input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));st.input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));}
    persist();const editor=$('evpaCustomEffectEditor');if(editor){const preview=$('evcePreview');if(preview){try{preview.style.backgroundImage=window.getComputedStyle(preview).backgroundImage;}catch(_){}}}
  }
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(!gradientPickState)return;if(e.target?.closest?.('#ethvelleColorPicker .evpa-picker-close,#ethvelleColorPicker .evpa-picker-apply,#ethvelleColorPicker .evcp-close,#ethvelleColorPicker .evcp-apply,[onclick*="closeEthvelleColorPicker"]')||e.target?.id==='ethvelleColorPicker')setTimeout(finishGradientColorPick,80);},true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&gradientPickState)setTimeout(finishGradientColorPick,80);},true);
  function decorateGradientColorInputs(editor){
    editor.querySelectorAll('.evce-stop input[data-stop-color]').forEach(input=>{
      if(input.dataset.evceWheelReady==='1')return;input.dataset.evceWheelReady='1';
      const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='evce-wheel-color';b.style.background=input.value;b.title='Choose stop color';b.setAttribute('aria-label','Choose color for this gradient stop');b.onclick=()=>window.openEthvelleGradientStopColor(b);input.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',b);
      const observer=new MutationObserver(()=>{b.style.background=input.value;});observer.observe(input,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['value']});input.addEventListener('input',()=>b.style.background=input.value);input.addEventListener('change',()=>b.style.background=input.value);
    });
    ['evceTexColor','evceTexColor2'].forEach(id=>{const input=$(id);if(!input||input.dataset.evceWheelReady==='1')return;input.dataset.evceWheelReady='1';const wrap=input.parentElement;const label=wrap?.querySelector('label')?.textContent||'Color';const btn=document.createElement('button');btn.type='button';btn.className='evce-wheel-color';btn.style.background=input.value;btn.title='Choose '+label.toLowerCase();btn.setAttribute('aria-label','Choose '+label.toLowerCase());btn.onclick=()=>window.openEthvelleGradientStopColor(btn);wrap?.appendChild(btn);input.addEventListener('input',()=>btn.style.background=input.value);input.addEventListener('change',()=>btn.style.background=input.value);});
  }
  function tweakEditor(){const editor=$('evpaCustomEffectEditor');if(editor&&editor.classList.contains('active'))decorateGradientColorInputs(editor);}
  function boot(){
    buildImageTextureInput();ensureFormatTab();refreshLayout(true);tweakEditor();
    const mo=new MutationObserver(records=>{
      let needsLayoutRefresh=false;
      for(const record of records){
        const raw=record.target;
        const target=raw&&raw.nodeType===1?raw:raw&&raw.parentElement;
        // Tab switches rebuild evpaControls synchronously. Scheduling another global refresh
        // for those same nodes caused duplicate work and made Shadow/Bend/Format feel delayed.
        if(target&&target.closest&&target.closest('#evpaControls,#evpaTabs,#evpaPalette,#ethvelleTextComposer,#textPanelBody,#timelineInner,#overlayMediaLayer'))continue;
        needsLayoutRefresh=true;break;
      }
      if(needsLayoutRefresh)scheduleRefresh();
      tweakEditor();
    });
    mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
    const oldSetValue=window.ethvellePicsartSetValue;
    if(typeof oldSetValue==='function'&&!oldSetValue.__evvelleV18){const wrap=function(key,value){const out=oldSetValue.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='fontFamily'){const g=$('evpaControls');g?.querySelectorAll('.evpa-font-card').forEach(c=>c.classList.toggle('active',c.dataset.font===String(value)));}if(key==='textTransform')applyTextTransforms();return out;};wrap.__evvelleV18=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetValue=wrap;}
    const oldSetTexture=window.ethvellePicsartSetTexture;
    if(typeof oldSetTexture==='function'&&!oldSetTexture.__evvelleV18){const wrap=function(css,size){const out=oldSetTexture.apply(this,arguments);if(String(css).includes('data:image/')){try{const key='ethvelleCustomTextTexturesV1',list=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');if(!list.some(x=>x.css===css)){list.unshift({name:'Image texture',css,size:size||'100% 100%'});localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(list.slice(0,5)));}}catch(_){}}return out;};wrap.__evvelleV18=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetTexture=wrap;}
    const oldSave=window.saveEthvelleTextComposer;
    if(typeof oldSave==='function'&&!oldSave.__evvelleV18){const saveWrap=function(){const before=getTarget();const savedTransform=before?.textTransform||'none';const out=oldSave.apply(this,arguments);let arr=[];try{arr=typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:(window.overlays||[]);}catch(_){arr=window.overlays||[];}const clip=(arr||[]).find(o=>o&&o.type==='text'&&String(o.id)===String(window.selectedClip));if(clip){clip.textTransform=savedTransform;persist();}return out;};saveWrap.__evvelleV18=true;window.saveEthvelleTextComposer=saveWrap;}
    applyTextTransforms();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-update-v11-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
  window.ethvelleRigOverlayVisible = false;

  function drawRigDot(g,p,r){
    if(!p || !Number.isFinite(p.x) || !Number.isFinite(p.y)) return;
    g.beginPath();g.arc(p.x,p.y,r,0,Math.PI*2);
    g.fillStyle='#2F5CE8';g.fill();g.lineWidth=1.15;g.strokeStyle='#FFFFFF';g.stroke();
  }
  window.drawEthvelleRigOverlay = function(g, character){
    if(!g || !character) return;
    g.save();
    g.strokeStyle='#2F5CE8';g.fillStyle='#2F5CE8';g.globalAlpha=.96;
    g.lineWidth=1.45;g.lineCap='round';g.lineJoin='round';
    let points=[];
    try{
      if(character.sheet && typeof sheetRigForCharacter==='function' && typeof sheetSolveRig==='function'){
        const rig=sheetRigForCharacter(character);
        const solved=sheetSolveRig(rig.bones,rig.baseAngles);
        Object.keys(solved||{}).forEach(key=>{
          if(key==='root') return;
          const bone=solved[key];if(!bone||!bone.s||!bone.e) return;
          g.beginPath();g.moveTo(bone.s.x,bone.s.y);g.lineTo(bone.e.x,bone.e.y);g.stroke();
        });
        // Never draw dead-end dots. The same 23-point list is used by getJointList() for
        // pointer hit-testing and dragSheetCharacterJoint() for the corresponding control.
        if(typeof getJointList==='function') (getJointList()||[]).forEach(j=>points.push(j));
      }else if(typeof getBoneStart==='function' && typeof getBoneEnd==='function'){
        ['spine','head','armL','armL_lo','armR','armR_lo','legL','legL_lo','legR','legR_lo'].forEach(key=>{
          try{const a=getBoneStart(key),b=getBoneEnd(key);if(a&&b){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(a.x,a.y);g.lineTo(b.x,b.y);g.stroke();points.push(a,b);}}catch(_e){}
        });
        if(typeof getJointList==='function') (getJointList()||[]).forEach(j=>points.push(j));
      }
      const uniq=new Map();
      points.forEach(p=>{if(p&&Number.isFinite(p.x)&&Number.isFinite(p.y))uniq.set(Math.round(p.x*10)+':'+Math.round(p.y*10),p);});
      uniq.forEach(p=>drawRigDot(g,p,3.05));
    }catch(err){console.warn('Rig overlay could not be drawn',err);}
    g.restore();
  };

  function ensureRigTool(){
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip');if(!row||$('clipRigOverlayTool'))return;
    const btn=document.createElement('div');btn.id='clipRigOverlayTool';btn.className='tool-item evpa-rig-tool';
    btn.setAttribute('role','button');btn.tabIndex=0;btn.style.display='none';
    btn.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="5" cy="5" r="2"/><circle cx="19" cy="5" r="2"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2"/><circle cx="5" cy="19" r="2"/><circle cx="19" cy="19" r="2"/><path d="M7 5h10M6.5 6.5l4 4M17.5 6.5l-4 4M7 19h10M6.5 17.5l4-4M17.5 17.5l-4-4"/></svg><span>Show rig</span>';
    const toggle=()=>{
      window.ethvelleRigOverlayVisible=!window.ethvelleRigOverlayVisible;
      syncRigToolState();
      if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();
    };
    btn.addEventListener('click',toggle);
    btn.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();toggle();}});
    const anchor=$('clipTransformTool')||$('clipForwardTool');
    if(anchor&&anchor.parentNode===row)row.insertBefore(btn,anchor.nextSibling);else row.appendChild(btn);
  }
  function syncRigToolState(){
    const btn=$('clipRigOverlayTool');if(!btn)return;
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');
    const charLayer=/^char\d+$/.test(id),animClip=id==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(id);
    const canShow=!!(typeof activeCharacter==='function'&&activeCharacter())&&(charLayer||animClip);
    btn.style.display=canShow?'flex':'none';
    btn.classList.toggle('active',!!window.ethvelleRigOverlayVisible);
    const label=btn.querySelector('span');if(label)label.textContent=window.ethvelleRigOverlayVisible?'Hide rig':'Show rig';
    btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed',window.ethvelleRigOverlayVisible?'true':'false');
  }

  // Replace ordering only for the combined overlay collection (text + image/video/audio).
  // Backgrounds are explicitly routed back to the original dedicated time-slot reorder logic.
  const priorReorder=window.reorderSelectedLayer;
  window.reorderSelectedLayer=function(dir){
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');
    if(!id)return;
    const isBackground=id.startsWith('bg') || (typeof backgroundClipForSelection==='function'&&!!backgroundClipForSelection());
    if(isBackground){if(typeof priorReorder==='function')return priorReorder.apply(this,arguments);return;}
    if(/^char\d+$/.test(id)){if(typeof priorReorder==='function')return priorReorder.apply(this,arguments);return;}
    if(id==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(id)){return;}
    let idx=-1;
    try{idx=(overlays||[]).findIndex(o=>String(o&&o.id)===id);}catch(_e){}
    if(idx<0){if(typeof priorReorder==='function')return priorReorder.apply(this,arguments);return;}
    const next=idx+(Number(dir)>=0?1:-1);
    if(next<0||next>=(overlays||[]).length){
      if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(Number(dir)>=0?'Already at the front':'Already at the back',{duration:1200});
      return;
    }
    [overlays[idx],overlays[next]]=[overlays[next],overlays[idx]];
    if(typeof renderOverlayLayers==='function')renderOverlayLayers();
    if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
    if(typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();
    if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
  };

  // The text dock hides the timeline toolbar while open, so give selected text a dedicated
  // plus/New text action in that same dock. A non-matching sentinel ID forces a truly blank
  // composer even if an existing text layer remains selected underneath.
  function ensureTextNewButton(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(dock)dock.querySelectorAll('.evpa-new-layer-btn').forEach(b=>b.remove());
    document.querySelectorAll('.ethvelle-text-row-plus').forEach(b=>b.remove());
  }

  const tabsWithoutPalette=new Set(['spacing','opacity','styles','perspective','bend','format','gradient','texture']);
  function syncTextPalette(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(!dock)return;
    const active=dock.querySelector('#evpaTabs .evpa-tab.active');
    const tab=active?String(active.dataset.tab||'color'):'color';
    dock.classList.toggle('evpa-no-palette',tabsWithoutPalette.has(tab));
    const palette=$('evpaPalette');if(palette)palette.setAttribute('aria-hidden',tabsWithoutPalette.has(tab)?'true':'false');
  }

  const priorSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  let evltMoveBadgeState=null;
  function selectedMoveItem(){
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');
    if(!id)return null;
    if(/^char\d+$/.test(id)){
      const index=parseInt(id.slice(4),10),item=(typeof characters!=='undefined'&&characters)?characters[index]:null;
      return item?{kind:'character',item,index}:null;
    }
    try{
      const bg=typeof backgroundClipForSelection==='function'?backgroundClipForSelection():null;
      if(bg){const list=typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[];return {kind:'background',item:bg,index:list.indexOf(bg)};}
    }catch(_e){}
    try{
      const list=typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];
      const index=list.findIndex(item=>String(item&&item.id)===id);
      if(index>=0)return {kind:'overlay',item:list[index],index:list[index].__ethvelleBehindBackground?-1:index};
    }catch(_e){}
    return null;
  }
  function paintMoveBadge(buttonId,count,direction){
    const button=$(buttonId);if(!button)return;
    let badge=button.querySelector('.evlt-move-count-badge');
    if(!badge){badge=document.createElement('span');badge.className='evlt-move-count-badge';badge.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');button.appendChild(badge);}
    const value=Math.max(0,Math.abs(Number(count)||0));
    badge.textContent=value?String(value):'';
    badge.classList.toggle('active',value>0);
    if(value)badge.title=`Moved ${value} clip${value===1?'':'s'} ${direction}`;else badge.removeAttribute('title');
  }
  function refreshMoveBadges(){
    const target=selectedMoveItem();
    if(!target||target.index<0){evltMoveBadgeState=null;paintMoveBadge('clipForwardTool',0,'forward');paintMoveBadge('clipBackwardTool',0,'backward');return;}
    if(!evltMoveBadgeState||evltMoveBadgeState.item!==target.item||evltMoveBadgeState.kind!==target.kind){
      evltMoveBadgeState={item:target.item,kind:target.kind,originIndex:target.index};
    }
    const delta=target.index-evltMoveBadgeState.originIndex;
    paintMoveBadge('clipForwardTool',Math.max(0,delta),'forward');
    paintMoveBadge('clipBackwardTool',Math.max(0,-delta),'backward');
  }
  window.syncBottomToolsMode=function(){
    const result=typeof priorSync==='function'?priorSync.apply(this,arguments):undefined;
    ensureRigTool();ensureTextNewButton();syncRigToolState();syncTextPalette();
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');
    let ov=null;try{ov=(overlays||[]).find(o=>String(o&&o.id)===id)||null;}catch(_e){}
    const background=id.startsWith('bg')||(typeof backgroundClipForSelection==='function'&&!!backgroundClipForSelection());
    const charLayer=/^char\d+$/.test(id);
    const canReorder=!!ov||background||charLayer;
    ['clipForwardTool','clipBackwardTool'].forEach(toolId=>{const el=$(toolId);if(el)el.style.display=id&&canReorder?'flex':'none';});
    refreshMoveBadges();
    return result;
  };

  // Only keep the rig overlay active while a character animation/layer is selected.
  const priorSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof priorSelect==='function')window.selectClipForChrome=function(which){
    const id=String(which==null?'':which);
    if(!(id==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(id)||/^char\d+$/.test(id)))window.ethvelleRigOverlayVisible=false;
    const result=priorSelect.apply(this,arguments);
    ensureRigTool();ensureTextNewButton();syncRigToolState();syncTextPalette();
    return result;
  };
  const priorDeselect=window.deselectAllClips;
  if(typeof priorDeselect==='function')window.deselectAllClips=function(){window.ethvelleRigOverlayVisible=false;const result=priorDeselect.apply(this,arguments);syncRigToolState();return result;};

  // Re-synchronize the palette after tab changes, including the dynamically-added Format tab.
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaTabs .evpa-tab'))setTimeout(syncTextPalette,0);
  },true);
  const tabHost=$('evpaTabs');
  if(tabHost){const tabObserver=new MutationObserver(syncTextPalette);tabObserver.observe(tabHost,{subtree:true,childList:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','data-tab']});}

  // Keep panel gestures inside the panel. Stopping bubbling at the panel root does not cancel
  // native slider/input taps, but prevents global canvas/timeline drag handlers from stealing them.
  function wireTouchSafePanel(el){
    if(!el||el.dataset.evTouchSafe==='1')return;el.dataset.evTouchSafe='1';
    el.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>e.stopPropagation(),{passive:true});
    el.addEventListener('touchstart',e=>e.stopPropagation(),{passive:true});
  }
  function boot(){
    ensureRigTool();ensureTextNewButton();syncRigToolState();syncTextPalette();
    wireTouchSafePanel($('ethvellePicsartTextDock'));
    wireTouchSafePanel($('ethvelleColorPicker'));
    wireTouchSafePanel($('ethvelleTextComposer'));
    wireTouchSafePanel($('evpaCustomEffectEditor'));
    wireTouchSafePanel($('ethvelleTextStylePanel'));
    const mo=new MutationObserver(()=>{
      ensureRigTool();ensureTextNewButton();syncTextPalette();
      wireTouchSafePanel($('ethvellePicsartTextDock'));
      wireTouchSafePanel($('ethvelleColorPicker'));
      wireTouchSafePanel($('ethvelleTextComposer'));
      wireTouchSafePanel($('evpaCustomEffectEditor'));
      wireTouchSafePanel($('ethvelleTextStylePanel'));
    });
    mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>




<!-- Ethvelle CapCut selection, timeline gestures, and touch-response repair v12 -->
<style id="ethvelle-update-v12-css">
  /* Keep the existing CapCut-style 20px white side tabs and 2px gray grip line exactly as-is,
     but center each tab on the actual clip edge so half extends beyond the clip. The selected
     track/row must allow that visible overhang; otherwise overflow:hidden silently crops it. */
  
  #timelineInner .anim-clip.selected,
  #timelineInner .overlay-clip.selected,
  #timelineInner .track-clip.selected{
    z-index:30!important;
  }
  /* Text/audio rules elsewhere in the file had higher specificity and were forcing left:0/right:0.
     Reassert the exact same outward geometry for every overlay clip type without thinning the tabs. */
  

  /* The stage's selected-text frame stays a clean white rectangle; remove only the extra black
     shadow/keyline on that text frame (other non-text transform frames keep their normal style). */
  #selBoxLayer.ethvelle-text-selected-box{box-shadow:none!important;filter:none!important;outline:none!important;}
  #selBoxLayer.ethvelle-text-selected-box .sb-frame,
  #selBoxLayer.ethvelle-text-selected-box .sb-frame::before,
  #selBoxLayer.ethvelle-text-selected-box .sb-frame::after{
    border:1.5px solid rgba(255,255,255,.98)!important;border-radius:0!important;
    box-shadow:none!important;outline:none!important;filter:none!important;text-shadow:none!important;
  }
  #selBoxLayer.ethvelle-text-selected-box .sb-frame::before,
  #selBoxLayer.ethvelle-text-selected-box .sb-frame::after{content:none!important;display:none!important;}

  /* Remove the redundant plus badge and the dock-level New text button. */
  #timelineInner .overlay-track-row[data-group="text"] .ethvelle-text-row-plus,
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-new-layer-btn{display:none!important;}
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaTabs [data-tab="background"]{display:none!important;}

  /* Nested horizontal preset/palette rows must be allowed to receive touch gestures. Earlier
     pan-y on their parent intersected with pan-x on the row, producing 'touch takes 3 tries'. */
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock.evpa-dock{touch-action:auto!important;}
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-controls{touch-action:auto!important;}
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-palette{touch-action:auto!important;}
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-swatches,
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-font-gallery,
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-choice-row{touch-action:pan-x!important;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;}
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-gradient-presets,
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-style-presets{touch-action:pan-y!important;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;}
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock input[type="range"],
  #ethvelleTextStylePanel input[type="range"],
  #ethvelleTextComposer input[type="range"],
  #evpaCustomEffectEditor input[type="range"],
  #ethvelleColorPicker input[type="range"]{touch-action:none!important;cursor:ew-resize;user-select:none;-webkit-user-select:none;}

  /* A separate backplate pseudo-element makes Highlight visible even while gradient/texture is
     applied to the text fill. Its color, opacity and radius are set from the selected text layer. */
  #overlayMediaLayer .ethvelle-overlay-text-content.ev-text-highlight-plate{
    position:relative!important;isolation:isolate!important;background-color:transparent!important;
  }
  #overlayMediaLayer .ethvelle-overlay-text-content.ev-text-highlight-plate::before{
    content:""!important;display:block!important;position:absolute!important;inset:0!important;
    z-index:-1!important;pointer-events:none!important;
    background:var(--ev-text-highlight-fill,rgba(24,42,102,.78))!important;
    border-radius:var(--ev-text-highlight-radius,8px)!important;
    box-shadow:none!important;outline:none!important;
  }
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-update-v12-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(n,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,n));
  const scrollSelectors=[
    '#evpaPalette .evpa-swatches',
    '#evpaControls .evpa-gradient-presets',
    '#evpaControls .evpa-font-gallery',
    '#evpaControls .evpa-choice-row',
    '#evpaControls .evpa-style-presets'
  ];
  function captureDockScroll(){
    const root=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(!root)return [];
    const state=[];
    scrollSelectors.forEach(sel=>Array.from(root.querySelectorAll(sel)).forEach((el,i)=>state.push({sel,i,left:el.scrollLeft,top:el.scrollTop})));
    const tabs=$('evpaTabs');if(tabs)state.push({sel:'#evpaTabs',i:0,left:tabs.scrollLeft,top:tabs.scrollTop});
    return state;
  }
  function restoreDockScroll(state){
    const root=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(!root)return;
    state.forEach(item=>{
      const host=item.sel==='#evpaTabs' ? $('evpaTabs') : root;
      if(!host)return;
      const nodes=item.sel==='#evpaTabs'?[host]:Array.from(host.querySelectorAll(item.sel));
      const el=nodes[item.i];if(el){el.scrollLeft=item.left;el.scrollTop=item.top;}
    });
  }
  function wrapPreservingScroll(name,skip){
    const original=window[name];if(typeof original!=='function'||original.__evvelleV12Scroll)return;
    const wrapped=function(){
      if(skip&&skip.apply(this,arguments))return original.apply(this,arguments);
      const saved=captureDockScroll();
      const result=original.apply(this,arguments);
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>restoreDockScroll(saved));
      setTimeout(()=>restoreDockScroll(saved),24);
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evvelleV12Scroll=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  }

  function textOverlays(){
    try{return (typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:(window.overlays||[])).filter(o=>o&&o.type==='text');}
    catch(_e){return (window.overlays||[]).filter(o=>o&&o.type==='text');}
  }
  function rgba(hex,opacity){
    let h=String(hex||'#182A66').trim();
    if(/^#[0-9a-f]{3}$/i.test(h))h='#'+h.slice(1).split('').map(c=>c+c).join('');
    if(!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(h))h='#182A66';
    const rgb=[1,3,5].map(i=>parseInt(h.slice(i,i+2),16));
    return 'rgba('+rgb[0]+','+rgb[1]+','+rgb[2]+','+(clamp(Number(opacity==null?78:opacity),0,100)/100)+')';
  }
  function textNodeFor(ov){
    try{
      const map=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'?overlayMediaEls:null;
      let host=map&&map[ov.id];
      if(!host){const layer=$('overlayMediaLayer');if(layer)host=Array.from(layer.children).find(n=>String(n.dataset.overlayId||n.dataset.ovId||'')===String(ov.id));}
      return host&&host.querySelector('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content');
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function syncHighlightPlates(){
    textOverlays().forEach(ov=>{
      const node=textNodeFor(ov);if(!node)return;
      const enabled=!!(ov.textBackgroundEnabled||ov.textHighlightEnabled);
      node.classList.toggle('ev-text-highlight-plate',enabled);
      if(!enabled){node.style.removeProperty('--ev-text-highlight-fill');node.style.removeProperty('--ev-text-highlight-radius');return;}
      const col=ov.textBackgroundColor||ov.textHighlightColor||'#182A66';
      const op=ov.textBackgroundOpacity==null?(ov.textHighlightOpacity==null?78:ov.textHighlightOpacity):ov.textBackgroundOpacity;
      const r=Math.max(0,Number(ov.textBackgroundRadius==null?(ov.textHighlightRadius==null?8:ov.textHighlightRadius):ov.textBackgroundRadius));
      node.style.setProperty('--ev-text-highlight-fill',rgba(col,op));
      node.style.setProperty('--ev-text-highlight-radius',r+'px');
    });
  }

  // Make an enabled highlight unmistakable on first use: provide visible defaults only when the
  // saved layer has no opacity/padding (or has zero), while preserving all later slider choices.
  const oldSetValue=window.ethvellePicsartSetValue;
  if(typeof oldSetValue==='function'&&!oldSetValue.__evvelleV12Highlight){
    const setWrap=function(key,value){
      if(key==='textBackgroundEnabled'&&!!value){
        const t=typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;
        if(t){
          if(t.textBackgroundOpacity==null||Number(t.textBackgroundOpacity)<=0)t.textBackgroundOpacity=82;
          if(t.textBackgroundPadding==null||Number(t.textBackgroundPadding)<=0)t.textBackgroundPadding=10;
          if(t.textBackgroundRadius==null)t.textBackgroundRadius=8;
          if(!t.textBackgroundColor)t.textBackgroundColor=t.textHighlightColor||'#182A66';
        }
      }
      const out=oldSetValue.apply(this,arguments);
      requestAnimationFrame(syncHighlightPlates);
      return out;
    };
    setWrap.__evvelleV12Highlight=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetValue=setWrap;
  }
  // Gradient / texture / style taps update in place now, so only the colour setters keep the restore.
  ['ethvellePicsartSetColor','ethvellePicsartSaveCustomColor']
    .forEach(n=>wrapPreservingScroll(n));
  // The value setter is also a frequent swatch/format action; wrap it separately while retaining
  // the Highlight defaults wrapper just installed above.
  wrapPreservingScroll('ethvellePicsartSetValue',function(key,value,unit,live){return !!live;});

  function stripUnwantedButtons(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(dock){
      dock.querySelectorAll('.evpa-new-layer-btn').forEach(n=>n.remove());
      const bg=dock.querySelector('#evpaTabs [data-tab="background"]');
      if(bg){
        const wasActive=bg.classList.contains('active');bg.remove();
        if(wasActive&&typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetTool==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetTool('highlight');
      }
    }
    document.querySelectorAll('.ethvelle-text-row-plus').forEach(n=>n.remove());
  }

  // Also catch text nodes created later by timeline rendering / project load.
  function boot(){
    stripUnwantedButtons();syncHighlightPlates();
    const layer=$('overlayMediaLayer');
    if(layer)new MutationObserver(()=>requestAnimationFrame(syncHighlightPlates)).observe(layer,{childList:true,subtree:true});
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(dock)new MutationObserver(()=>{stripUnwantedButtons();syncHighlightPlates();}).observe(dock,{childList:true,subtree:true});
    document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      if(e.target?.closest?.('#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaTabs .evpa-tab'))requestAnimationFrame(()=>{stripUnwantedButtons();syncHighlightPlates();});
    },true);
    window.addEventListener('resize',syncHighlightPlates,{passive:true});
    // renderOverlayMedia mutates existing text nodes in place; reapply only the isolated backplate
    // after it has set the layer's normal text styling.
    const oldRenderMedia=window.renderOverlayMedia;
    if(typeof oldRenderMedia==='function'&&!oldRenderMedia.__evvelleV12Plate){
      const renderWrap=function(){const out=oldRenderMedia.apply(this,arguments);syncHighlightPlates();return out;};
      renderWrap.__evvelleV12Plate=true;window.renderOverlayMedia=renderWrap;
    }
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-update-v13-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
  function textOverlays(){try{return (typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:(window.overlays||[])).filter(o=>o&&o.type==='text');}catch(_e){return (window.overlays||[]).filter(o=>o&&o.type==='text');}}
  function nodeFor(ov){
    let host=null;try{host=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls?overlayMediaEls[ov.id]:null;}catch(_e){}
    if(!host){const root=$('overlayMediaLayer');if(root)host=Array.from(root.children).find(n=>String(n.dataset.overlayId||n.dataset.ovId||'')===String(ov.id));}
    return host?.querySelector?.('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content')||null;
  }
  // The in-file applyEffectsToNode was updated to operate on a child glyph layer. This repair is a
  // guarded fallback for project elements produced before that child existed.
  function ensureGlyphLayers(){
    textOverlays().forEach(ov=>{
      const node=nodeFor(ov);if(!node)return;
      let glyph=node.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-text-effect-layer');
      if(!glyph){glyph=document.createElement('span');glyph.className='evpa-text-effect-layer';while(node.firstChild)glyph.appendChild(node.firstChild);node.appendChild(glyph);}
      const enabled=!!(ov.textBackgroundEnabled||ov.textHighlightEnabled);
      node.classList.toggle('ev-text-highlight-plate',enabled);
      if(enabled){
        let col=String(ov.textBackgroundColor||ov.textHighlightColor||'#182A66').trim();
        if(/^#[0-9a-f]{3}$/i.test(col))col='#'+col.slice(1).split('').map(c=>c+c).join('');
        if(!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(col))col='#182A66';
        const rgb=[1,3,5].map(i=>parseInt(col.slice(i,i+2),16));
        const opacity=ov.textBackgroundOpacity==null?(ov.textHighlightOpacity==null?78:ov.textHighlightOpacity):ov.textBackgroundOpacity;
        const pad=Math.max(0,Number(ov.textBackgroundPadding==null?(ov.textHighlightPadding||8):ov.textBackgroundPadding));
        const radius=Math.max(0,Number(ov.textBackgroundRadius==null?(ov.textHighlightRadius==null?8:ov.textHighlightRadius):ov.textBackgroundRadius));
        node.style.setProperty('--ev-text-highlight-fill',`rgba(${rgb[0]},${rgb[1]},${rgb[2]},${clamp(Number(opacity),0,100)/100})`);
        node.style.setProperty('--ev-text-highlight-padding',pad+'px');node.style.setProperty('--ev-text-highlight-inset',(-pad)+'px');node.style.setProperty('--ev-text-highlight-radius',radius+'px');
        if(window.ethvelleSyncTextBackplateShape)window.ethvelleSyncTextBackplateShape(node,ov);
      }else{node.style.removeProperty('--ev-text-highlight-fill');node.style.removeProperty('--ev-text-highlight-padding');node.style.removeProperty('--ev-text-highlight-inset');node.style.removeProperty('--ev-text-highlight-radius');node.style.removeProperty('--ev-text-highlight-mask');}
    });
  }
  // Swatch color changes now render once and update only the active ring. This also avoids reflowing
  // the color strip when the selected color is picked in Highlight or Stroke.
  const oldSetColor=window.ethvellePicsartSetColor;
  if(typeof oldSetColor==='function'&&!oldSetColor.__evvelleV13Fast){
    const fastSetColor=function(key,color){
      const value=String(color||'#FFFFFF').toUpperCase();
      const result=oldSetColor.apply(this,arguments);
      const palette=$('evpaPalette');if(palette)palette.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatch[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',String(b.dataset.color||'').toUpperCase()===value));
      requestAnimationFrame(ensureGlyphLayers);return result;
    };
    fastSetColor.__evvelleV13Fast=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=fastSetColor;
  }
  // Re-apply the separated plate after rendering and live control changes, without observing every
  // child mutation in the whole text dock (that was amplifying lag during color picking).
  ['renderFrame','renderOverlayLayers'].forEach(name=>{
    const original=window[name];if(typeof original!=='function'||original.__evvelleV13Isolated)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const result=original.apply(this,arguments);ensureGlyphLayers();return result;};
    wrapped.__evvelleV13Isolated=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  });
  ['ethvellePicsartSetValue','ethvellePicsartSetGradient','ethvellePicsartSetTexture','ethvellePicsartApplyCustomGradient','ethvellePicsartApplyCustomTexture','ethvellePicsartApplyStyle'].forEach(name=>{
    const original=window[name];if(typeof original!=='function'||original.__evvelleV13Refresh)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const result=original.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(ensureGlyphLayers);return result;};
    wrapped.__evvelleV13Refresh=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  });
  function boot(){ensureGlyphLayers();}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-update-v14-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const priorSetColor=window.ethvellePicsartSetColor;
  const normalizeHex=value=>{
    let h=String(value||'#FFFFFF').trim().toUpperCase();
    if(/^#[0-9A-F]{3}$/.test(h))h='#'+h.slice(1).split('').map(ch=>ch+ch).join('');
    return /^#[0-9A-F]{6}$/.test(h)?h:'#FFFFFF';
  };
  const rgba=(hex,alpha)=>{
    const h=normalizeHex(hex).slice(1),a=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(alpha==null?100:alpha)))/100;
    return `rgba(${parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16)},${parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16)},${parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16)},${a})`;
  };
  function getTextNode(t){
    if(!t)return null;let host=null;
    try{if(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)host=overlayMediaEls[t.id]||null;}catch(_e){}
    if(!host){const root=$('overlayMediaLayer');if(root)host=Array.from(root.children).find(n=>String(n.dataset.overlayId||n.dataset.ovId||'')===String(t.id))||null;}
    return host?.querySelector?.('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content')||null;
  }
  function plateStyle(node,t,colorOverride){
    if(!node||!t)return;
    const enabled=!!(t.textBackgroundEnabled||t.textHighlightEnabled);
    node.classList.toggle('ev-text-highlight-plate',enabled);
    if(!enabled){['--ev-text-highlight-fill','--ev-text-highlight-padding','--ev-text-highlight-inset','--ev-text-highlight-radius'].forEach(k=>node.style.removeProperty(k));return;}
    const col=normalizeHex(colorOverride||t.textBackgroundColor||t.textHighlightColor||'#182A66');
    const op=t.textBackgroundOpacity==null?(t.textHighlightOpacity==null?78:t.textHighlightOpacity):t.textBackgroundOpacity;
    const pad=Math.max(0,Number(t.textBackgroundPadding==null?(t.textHighlightPadding==null?8:t.textHighlightPadding):t.textBackgroundPadding));
    const radius=Math.max(0,Number(t.textBackgroundRadius==null?(t.textHighlightRadius==null?8:t.textHighlightRadius):t.textBackgroundRadius));
    const h=col.slice(1),n=[0,2,4].map(i=>parseInt(h.slice(i,i+2),16));
    node.style.setProperty('--ev-text-highlight-fill',`rgba(${n[0]},${n[1]},${n[2]},${Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(op==null?78:op)))/100})`);
    node.style.setProperty('--ev-text-highlight-padding',pad+'px');node.style.setProperty('--ev-text-highlight-inset',(-pad)+'px');node.style.setProperty('--ev-text-highlight-radius',radius+'px');
    if(window.ethvelleSyncTextBackplateShape)window.ethvelleSyncTextBackplateShape(node,t);
  }
  let colorSaveTimer=null;
  function persistNow(){
    if(colorSaveTimer!==null){clearTimeout(colorSaveTimer);colorSaveTimer=null;}
    try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}catch(_e){try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}catch(__){}}
  }
  function persistOne(){
    if(colorSaveTimer!==null)clearTimeout(colorSaveTimer);
    colorSaveTimer=setTimeout(()=>{colorSaveTimer=null;try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}catch(_e){try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}catch(__){}}},100);
  }
  window.addEventListener('pagehide',persistNow,{passive:true});
  // Swatch changes are applied to the already-rendered node instead of rerendering the whole stage.
  // This prevents slow Highlight/Stroke palette taps and keeps the swatch row at its exact scroll offset.
  window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=function(key,color){
    const value=normalizeHex(color),t=typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;
    if(!t){if(typeof priorSetColor==='function')return priorSetColor.apply(this,arguments);return;}
    const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer')?.classList.contains('active');
    if(composer&&typeof priorSetColor==='function'){return priorSetColor.apply(this,arguments);}
    t[key]=value;
    if(key==='textColor'){
      t.textGradient='';t.textTexture='';t.textTextureSize='100% 100%';
      if(Number(t.textOpacity)===0)t.textOpacity=100;
    }
    const activeTab=$('evpaTabs')?.querySelector('.evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab||'';
    if(key==='textBackgroundColor'&&(activeTab==='highlight'||activeTab==='background'))t.textHighlightColor=value;
    const node=getTextNode(t),glyph=node?.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-text-effect-layer');
    if(node){
      if(key==='textColor'){
        const fill=rgba(value,t.textOpacity);
        node.style.setProperty('color',fill,'important');
        node.style.setProperty('background-image','none','important');
        node.style.setProperty('background-clip','border-box','important');
        node.style.setProperty('-webkit-background-clip','border-box','important');
        if(glyph){
          glyph.style.setProperty('color',fill,'important');
          glyph.style.setProperty('-webkit-text-fill-color',fill,'important');
          glyph.style.setProperty('background-image','none','important');
          glyph.style.setProperty('background-clip','border-box','important');
          glyph.style.setProperty('-webkit-background-clip','border-box','important');
          glyph.style.setProperty('background-size','auto','important');
          glyph.style.setProperty('opacity','1','important');
          glyph.querySelectorAll('span').forEach(span=>{
            span.style.setProperty('color',fill,'important');
            span.style.setProperty('-webkit-text-fill-color',fill,'important');
            span.style.setProperty('background-image','none','important');
            span.style.removeProperty('background-clip');
            span.style.removeProperty('-webkit-background-clip');
            span.style.removeProperty('background-size');
            span.style.removeProperty('background-position');
            span.style.removeProperty('background-repeat');
          });
        }
      }else if(key==='strokeColor'){
        const width=Math.max(0,Number(t.strokeWidth)||0),stroke=width>0?`${width}px ${rgba(value,t.strokeOpacity)}`:'0 transparent';
        if(glyph){glyph.style.webkitTextStroke=stroke;glyph.style.paintOrder='stroke fill';}
        node.style.webkitTextStroke='0 transparent';node.style.paintOrder='normal';
      }else if(key==='shadowColor'){
        const shadow=t.shadowEnabled?`${(Number(t.shadowX)||0)}px ${Math.max(4,Number(t.shadowY)||6)}px ${Math.max(0,Number(t.shadowBlur)||0)}px ${rgba(value,t.shadowOpacity)}`:'none';
        if(glyph)window.ethvelleApplyTextShadow(glyph,shadow,!!(String(t.textGradient||'').trim()||String(t.textTexture||'').trim()));else node.style.textShadow=shadow;
        node.style.textShadow='none';
      }else if(key==='textBackgroundColor'){
        plateStyle(node,t,value);
      }
    }
    const palette=$('evpaPalette');if(palette)palette.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatch[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',normalizeHex(b.dataset.color)===value));
    persistOne();
  };
  // Refresh glyph-only shadow/stroke when a model redraw changes the numeric controls.
  function syncGlyphPaint(){
    let list=[];try{list=typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:(window.overlays||[]);}catch(_e){list=window.overlays||[];}
    list.filter(o=>o&&o.type==='text').forEach(t=>{
      const node=getTextNode(t);if(!node)return;const glyph=node.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-text-effect-layer');if(!glyph)return;
      const strokeWidth=Math.max(0,Number(t.strokeWidth)||0);
      glyph.style.webkitTextStroke=strokeWidth>0?`${strokeWidth}px ${rgba(t.strokeColor||'#000000',t.strokeOpacity)}`:'0 transparent';
      glyph.style.paintOrder='stroke fill';
      window.ethvelleApplyTextShadow(glyph,t.shadowEnabled?`${(Number(t.shadowX)||0)}px ${Math.max(4,Number(t.shadowY)||6)}px ${Math.max(0,Number(t.shadowBlur)||0)}px ${rgba(t.shadowColor||'#000000',t.shadowOpacity)}`:'none',!!(String(t.textGradient||'').trim()||String(t.textTexture||'').trim()));
      node.style.webkitTextStroke='0 transparent';node.style.paintOrder='normal';node.style.textShadow='none';
      if(t.textBackgroundEnabled||t.textHighlightEnabled)plateStyle(node,t);
    });
  }
  ['renderFrame','renderOverlayLayers'].forEach(name=>{
    const original=window[name];if(typeof original!=='function'||original.__evvelleV14Paint)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const result=original.apply(this,arguments);syncGlyphPaint();return result;};
    wrapped.__evvelleV14Paint=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  });
  // Keep section palette visibility accurate on both startup and tab changes, without rebuilding it.
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{const tab=e.target?.closest?.('#evpaTabs .evpa-tab');if(!tab)return;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(!dock)return;const font=tab.dataset.tab==='font';if(font)dock.classList.remove('evpa-no-palette');});},true);
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',syncGlyphPaint,{once:true});else syncGlyphPaint();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v19-contextual-toolbar">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const byId=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  function selectedId(){
    try{return String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:(window.selectedClip??''));}
    catch(_e){return String(window.selectedClip??'');}
  }
  function selectedOverlay(){
    const id=selectedId();
    try{
      const list=typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:(window.overlays||[]);
      return (list||[]).find(item=>item&&String(item.id)===id)||null;
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function setTextEditVisibility(){
    const edit=byId('clipTextEditTool');
    if(!edit)return;
    const clip=selectedOverlay();
    const isText=!!clip&&clip.type==='text';
    // This is the final toolbar sync layer, after legacy toolbar scripts have run.
    // Only a selected text clip is allowed to expose the pencil/text-edit control.
    edit.style.display=isText?'flex':'none';
    edit.setAttribute('aria-hidden',isText?'false':'true');
  }
  function setContextualAddAction(){
    const add=byId('clipAddMediaTool');
    if(!add||add.dataset.evV19ContextAdd==='1')return;
    add.dataset.evV19ContextAdd='1';
    // Replaces the old inline handler rather than adding a second click listener, so a text
    // selection cannot accidentally launch the device media picker as well.
    add.onclick=function(event){
      if(event){event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();}
      const clip=selectedOverlay();
      if(clip&&clip.type==='text'){
        if(typeof window.openEthvelleTextComposer==='function'){
          window.openEthvelleTextComposer('__ethvelle_new_text_layer__');
        }
        return false;
      }
      const isAudio=!!clip&&clip.type==='audio';
      if(typeof window.triggerUpload==='function')window.triggerUpload(isAudio?'audio':'overlay',isAudio?'audio':'clips');
      return false;
    };
  }
  function refresh(){setContextualAddAction();setTextEditVisibility();}

  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__evV19ContextToolbar){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);refresh();return result;};
    wrapped.__evV19ContextToolbar=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  const oldSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect==='function'&&!oldSelect.__evV19ContextToolbar){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldSelect.apply(this,arguments);refresh();return result;};
    wrapped.__evV19ContextToolbar=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }
  const oldDeselect=window.deselectAllClips;
  if(typeof oldDeselect==='function'&&!oldDeselect.__evV19ContextToolbar){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldDeselect.apply(this,arguments);refresh();return result;};
    wrapped.__evV19ContextToolbar=true;window.deselectAllClips=wrapped;
  }
  document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    if(event.target&&event.target.closest&&event.target.closest('#timelineInner,#overlayRows'))requestAnimationFrame(refresh);
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',event=>{
    if(event.target&&event.target.closest&&event.target.closest('#timelineInner,#overlayRows'))requestAnimationFrame(refresh);
  },true);
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',refresh,{once:true});
  else refresh();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v20-selection-toolbar-guards">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  let rafPending=false;
  function currentId(){
    try{return String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:(window.selectedClip??''));}
    catch(_e){return String(window.selectedClip??'');}
  }
  function currentOverlay(id){
    try{
      const list=typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:(window.overlays||[]);
      return (list||[]).find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(id))||null;
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function textIsReallySelected(){
    const id=currentId();
    const selectedOverlayEl=document.querySelector('#timelineInner .overlay-clip.selected[data-overlay-id]');
    if(selectedOverlayEl){
      return selectedOverlayEl.dataset.group==='text' && String(selectedOverlayEl.dataset.overlayId)===id;
    }
    // If another kind of timeline clip is visibly selected, never trust an older selectedClip id.
    if(document.querySelector('#timelineInner .anim-clip.selected, #timelineInner .track-clip.selected'))return false;
    const overlay=currentOverlay(id);
    return !!overlay&&overlay.type==='text';
  }
  function syncPencil(){
    rafPending=false;
    const button=$('clipTextEditTool');
    const isText=textIsReallySelected();
    document.body.classList.toggle('ev-v20-text-layer-selected',isText);
    if(button){
      button.style.setProperty('display',isText?'flex':'none','important');
      button.setAttribute('aria-hidden',isText?'false':'true');
      button.tabIndex=isText?0:-1;
    }
  }
  function scheduleSync(){if(rafPending)return;rafPending=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(syncPencil));}
  // The original editor has multiple toolbar wrappers. Wrap the final versions so the pencil
  // rule is reapplied after each legacy toolbar update rather than racing it.
  ['syncBottomToolsMode','selectClipForChrome','deselectAllClips'].forEach(name=>{
    const old=window[name];
    if(typeof old!=='function'||old.__evV20PencilGuard)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const result=old.apply(this,arguments);scheduleSync();return result;};
    wrapped.__evV20PencilGuard=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  });
  ['click','pointerup','touchend'].forEach(type=>document.addEventListener(type,e=>{
    if(e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#timelineInner,#overlayRows,#overlayMediaLayer,#bottomToolsClip'))scheduleSync();
  },true));
  const timeline=$('timelineInner');
  if(timeline&&typeof MutationObserver!=='undefined'){
    const observer=new MutationObserver(records=>{
      if(records.some(r=>r.type==='childList'||(r.type==='attributes'&&r.attributeName==='class')))scheduleSync();
    });
    observer.observe(timeline,{subtree:true,childList:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  }
  // Keep the colored progress rail synchronized without drawing an outline around the rail.
  function updateRangeProgress(input){
    if(!input||input.type!=='range')return;
    const min=Number(input.min||0),max=Number(input.max||100),value=Number(input.value||0);
    const percent=max>min?Math.max(0,Math.min(100,(value-min)/(max-min)*100)):0;
    input.style.setProperty('--ev-range-progress',percent+'%');
  }
  function initRanges(root=document){root.querySelectorAll('#ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-range input[type="range"]').forEach(updateRangeProgress);}
  document.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.target&&e.target.matches&&e.target.matches('#ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-range input[type="range"]'))updateRangeProgress(e.target);},true);
  const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
  if(dock&&typeof MutationObserver!=='undefined')new MutationObserver(()=>initRanges(dock)).observe(dock,{subtree:true,childList:true});
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{initRanges();scheduleSync();},{once:true});
  else {initRanges();scheduleSync();}
})();
</script>

  /* Audio timeline clips and their inner artwork use true square corners. */

  #timelineInner .overlay-clip[data-group="audio"]::before,

<script id="ethvelle-text-polish-v21-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const rangeSelector='#ethvellePicsartTextDock input[type="range"],#ethvelleTextComposer input[type="range"],#ethvelleTextStylePanel input[type="range"],#ethvelleColorPicker input[type="range"],#evpaCustomEffectEditor input[type="range"],#textPanelBody .text-range-line input[type="range"],#evltEnhanceAmount,#chromaTolerance,#chromaSoftness,#ethvelleChromaColorPicker #evckLight';
  function updateProgress(input){
    if(!input||input.type!=='range')return;
    const min=Number(input.min===''?0:(input.min||0)),max=Number(input.max===''?100:(input.max||100)),value=Number(input.value||0);
    const pct=max>min?Math.max(0,Math.min(100,(value-min)/(max-min)*100)):0;
    const nextProgress=pct+'%';if(input.style.getPropertyValue('--ev-range-progress')!==nextProgress)input.style.setProperty('--ev-range-progress',nextProgress);
  }
  function initProgress(root=document){try{root.querySelectorAll(rangeSelector).forEach(updateProgress);}catch(_){}}
  document.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.target&&e.target.matches&&e.target.matches(rangeSelector))updateProgress(e.target);},true);
  document.addEventListener('change',e=>{if(e.target&&e.target.matches&&e.target.matches(rangeSelector))updateProgress(e.target);},true);

  function currentTarget(){try{return typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;}catch(_){return null;}}
  function currentColorKey(){try{return typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget():'textColor';}catch(_){return 'textColor';}}
  function norm(v){return String(v||'').trim().toUpperCase();}
  function syncColorMarkers(){
    const t=currentTarget();if(!t)return;
    const key=currentColorKey();const color=norm(t[key]||t.textColor||'#FFFFFF');
    const palette=$('evpaPalette');if(palette){/* v222-fix: one owner for palette markers. This used to ignore whether Shadow/Stroke/Background is switched on while ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches (feature-aware) did the opposite, so the two re-triggered each other forever and the active blue border blinked. */ if(typeof window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches==='function'){window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches();}else palette.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatch[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',norm(b.dataset.color)===color));}
    const composer=$('evtcColorStrip');if(composer)composer.querySelectorAll('.evtc-color-swatch[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',norm(b.dataset.color)===norm(t.textColor||'#FFFFFF')));
  }
  function syncEffectMarkers(){
    const controls=$('evpaControls'),tabs=$('evpaTabs'),t=currentTarget();if(!controls||!tabs||!t)return;
    const active=tabs.querySelector('.evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab;
    if(active!=='gradient'&&active!=='texture')return;
    const current=String(active==='gradient'?(t.textGradient||''):(t.textTexture||''));
    controls.querySelectorAll('.evpa-gradient-chip[data-evpa-effect][data-evpa-fill]').forEach(b=>{
      let fill='';try{fill=decodeURIComponent(b.dataset.evpaFill||'');}catch(_){fill=b.dataset.evpaFill||'';}
      b.classList.toggle('selected',b.dataset.evpaEffect===active&&fill===current);
    });
    // Saved image-texture chips can be created by the separate image-texture module.
    if(active==='texture'){
      let saved=[];try{saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ethvelleCustomTextTexturesV1')||'[]');}catch(_){saved=[];}
      controls.querySelectorAll('.evpa-image-texture-chip[data-image-texture-index]').forEach(b=>{
        const item=saved[Number(b.dataset.imageTextureIndex)];b.classList.toggle('selected',!!item&&String(item.css||'')===current);
      });
    }
  }
  function syncAll(){initProgress();syncColorMarkers();syncEffectMarkers();}

  const observedRoots=new WeakSet();
  function observeRoot(root,callback){
    if(!root||observedRoots.has(root)||typeof MutationObserver==='undefined')return;
    observedRoots.add(root);
    new MutationObserver(callback).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  }
  function bindObservers(){
    syncAll();
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(dock){dock.dataset.evV21Observed='1';observeRoot(dock,syncAll);}
    const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer');if(composer)observeRoot(composer,()=>{initProgress(composer);syncColorMarkers();});
    const picker=$('ethvelleColorPicker');if(picker)observeRoot(picker,()=>initProgress(picker));
    const editor=$('evpaCustomEffectEditor');if(editor)observeRoot(editor,()=>initProgress(editor));
    const textPanel=$('textPanelBody');if(textPanel)observeRoot(textPanel,()=>initProgress(textPanel));
    const originalSetColor=window.ethvellePicsartSetColor;
    if(typeof originalSetColor==='function'&&!originalSetColor.__evV21ColorSync){
      const wrapped=function(){const result=originalSetColor.apply(this,arguments);syncColorMarkers();return result;};wrapped.__evV21ColorSync=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=wrapped;
    }
    const originalComposerColor=window.setEthvelleComposerColor;
    if(typeof originalComposerColor==='function'&&!originalComposerColor.__evV21ColorSync){
      const wrapped=function(){const result=originalComposerColor.apply(this,arguments);syncColorMarkers();return result;};wrapped.__evV21ColorSync=true;window.setEthvelleComposerColor=wrapped;
    }
    const tabs=$('evpaTabs');if(tabs)tabs.querySelectorAll('.evpa-tab').forEach(btn=>{btn.style.touchAction='manipulation';});
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',bindObservers,{once:true});else bindObservers();
  // The dock may be created after this script when the app initializes; bind once when it appears.
  const bodyObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>{
    initProgress(document.body);
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(dock&&!dock.dataset.evV21Observed)bindObservers();
    const textPanel=$('textPanelBody');
    if(textPanel&&!textPanel.dataset.evV21Observed){
      textPanel.dataset.evV21Observed='1';observeRoot(textPanel,()=>initProgress(textPanel));initProgress(textPanel);
    }
  });
  if(document.body)bodyObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-text-section-update-v22-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const body=document.body;
  const app=document.getElementById('app');
  if(!body||!app)return;
  let locked=false;
  let originalHeight='',originalHeightPriority='',originalMinHeight='',originalMinHeightPriority='';
  function restoreProperty(el,name,value,priority){
    if(value)el.style.setProperty(name,value,priority||'');
    else el.style.removeProperty(name);
  }
  function syncComposerViewportLock(){
    const shouldLock=body.classList.contains('evtc-is-open');
    if(shouldLock&&!locked){
      locked=true;
      originalHeight=app.style.getPropertyValue('height');
      originalHeightPriority=app.style.getPropertyPriority('height');
      originalMinHeight=app.style.getPropertyValue('min-height');
      originalMinHeightPriority=app.style.getPropertyPriority('min-height');
      const height=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(window.innerHeight||document.documentElement.clientHeight||600));
      app.style.setProperty('height',height+'px','important');
      app.style.setProperty('min-height',height+'px','important');
    }else if(!shouldLock&&locked){
      restoreProperty(app,'height',originalHeight,originalHeightPriority);
      restoreProperty(app,'min-height',originalMinHeight,originalMinHeightPriority);
      locked=false;
    }
  }
  const classObserver=new MutationObserver(syncComposerViewportLock);
  classObserver.observe(body,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  syncComposerViewportLock();
  // If device orientation/window size changes before the keyboard takes focus, refresh the
  // locked height. While typing, leave it untouched so the keyboard cannot shrink the editor.
  window.addEventListener('resize',function(){
    if(!locked)return;
    const input=document.getElementById('evtcStageText');
    if(input&&document.activeElement===input)return;
    const height=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(window.innerHeight||document.documentElement.clientHeight||600));
    app.style.setProperty('height',height+'px','important');
    app.style.setProperty('min-height',height+'px','important');
  },{passive:true});
})();
</script>
