<script id="ethvelle-local-video-tools-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const defaults={brightness:100,contrast:100,saturation:100,hue:0,blur:0,grayscale:0,sepia:0};
  let enhanceRunning=false,enhanceRunToken=0,comparePercent=50,previewSyncTimer=0;
  function clamp(n,min,max){n=Number(n);return Number.isFinite(n)?Math.max(min,Math.min(max,n)):min;}
  function normalizeAdj(a){return Object.assign({},defaults,a||{});}
  function adjCss(a,enhanceAmount){
    // Enhance amount is handled by the per-frame processor, not a CSS filter.
    const x=normalizeAdj(a);
    const brightness=Number(x.brightness);
    const contrast=Number(x.contrast);
    const saturation=Number(x.saturation);
    const parts=[];
    // Do not attach a CSS filter at all when the clip is unchanged. On Android WebView,
    // even blur(0px) / a chain of identity filters can force low-resolution compositing.
    if(brightness!==100)parts.push(`brightness(${brightness}%)`);
    if(contrast!==100)parts.push(`contrast(${contrast}%)`);
    if(saturation!==100)parts.push(`saturate(${saturation}%)`);
    if(Number(x.hue)!==0)parts.push(`hue-rotate(${Number(x.hue)}deg)`);
    if(Number(x.blur)>0)parts.push(`blur(${Math.min(12,Number(x.blur))}px)`);
    if(Number(x.grayscale)>0)parts.push(`grayscale(${Math.min(100,Number(x.grayscale))}%)`);
    if(Number(x.sepia)>0)parts.push(`sepia(${Math.min(100,Number(x.sepia))}%)`);
    return parts.join(' ')||'none';
  }
  window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter=adjCss;
  function findBackgroundById(id){return (typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'?backgrounds:[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id))||null;}
  function findOverlayById(id){return (typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id))||null;}
  function unwrapSource(src){let v=String(src||'').trim();const m=v.match(/^url\((.*)\)$/i);if(m)v=m[1].trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g,'');return v;}
  function visualTarget(){
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'?'':(selectedClip||''));
    const bg=findBackgroundById(id);if(bg)return {kind:'background',model:bg};
    const ov=findOverlayById(id);if(ov&&(ov.type==='video'||ov.type==='image'))return {kind:'overlay',model:ov};
    if(!id){let active=null;try{active=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec==='number'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0):null;}catch(e){}if(active)return {kind:'background',model:active};}
    return null;
  }
  function enhanceTarget(){
    const target=visualTarget();if(!target||!target.model)return null;
    const src=unwrapSource(target.model.src);
    if(!src||!/^((blob:|data:)|https?:|file:)/i.test(src))return null;
    if(target.kind==='overlay'&&!['video','image'].includes(target.model.type))return null;
    if(target.kind==='background'&&String(target.model.src||'').trim()===src)return null;
    return {...target,src,type:target.kind==='background'?'image':target.model.type};
  }
  function migrateLegacyEnhanceDefault(model){
    // v2 initialized Enhance to 40% merely by opening the panel. Treat that unconfirmed
    // legacy value as neutral so previously edited clips do not suddenly look soft/filtered.
    if(model && !model.__evltEnhanceUserSet && Number(model.enhanceAmount)===40) model.enhanceAmount=0;
  }
  function updateTargetFilter(target){
    if(!target)return;
    migrateLegacyEnhanceDefault(target.model);
    const filter=adjCss(target.model.adjustments,target.model.enhanceAmount||0);
    if(target.kind==='overlay'){
      const el=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls[target.model.id])||null;if(el)el.style.filter=filter;
      const canvas=(typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'&&overlayChromaCanvases[target.model.id])||null;if(canvas)canvas.style.filter=filter;
    }else{const img=$('bgImgLayer');if(img)img.style.filter=filter;}
  }
  function refreshToolVisibility(){
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip');if(!row)return;
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'?'':(selectedClip||'')),ov=findOverlayById(id);
    const isTextOrAudio=!!ov&&(ov.type==='text'||ov.type==='audio');
    const isCharacter=!id||id==='main'||id.startsWith('seg')||id.startsWith('char')||(typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'&&selectedKfIdx!==null);
    const target=visualTarget(),enh=enhanceTarget();
    const showVisual=!!target&&!isTextOrAudio&&!isCharacter;
    const adj=$('clipAdjustTool');if(adj)adj.style.display=showVisual?'flex':'none';
    const en=$('clipEnhanceTool');if(en)en.style.display=!!enh&&!isTextOrAudio&&!isCharacter?'flex':'none';
    // Chroma and Extract Audio are the original clip-row controls; never create duplicates.
    const chroma=$('clipChromaTool'),audio=$('clipExtractAudioTool');
    const isVideo=!!ov&&ov.type==='video';
    if(chroma)chroma.style.display=isVideo?'flex':'none';if(audio)audio.style.display=isVideo?'flex':'none';
  }
  function ensureToolbar(){
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip');if(!row||$('clipAdjustTool'))return;
    const make=(id,label,icon,handler,title)=>{
      const b=document.createElement('div');b.className='tool-item evlt-inline-tool';b.id=id;b.setAttribute('role','button');b.setAttribute('tabindex','0');b.setAttribute('aria-label',label);b.title=title||label;b.innerHTML=`${icon}<span>${label}</span>`;
      b.addEventListener('click',handler);b.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();handler();}});return b;
    };
    const adjustIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M4 21v-7M4 10V3M12 21v-9M12 8V3M20 21v-5M20 12V3M2 14h4M10 8h4M18 16h4"/></svg>';
    const enhanceIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 16l4-5 4 3 4-6 4 8"/><rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="3"/></svg>';
    const adj=make('clipAdjustTool','Adjust',adjustIcon,()=>window.ethvelleOpenAdjustments(),'Adjust clip');
    const enh=make('clipEnhanceTool','Enhance',enhanceIcon,()=>window.ethvelleOpenEnhance(),'Enhance image or video');
    const anchor=$('clipForwardTool')||$('clipBackwardTool')||$('clipDuplicateTool');
    row.insertBefore(adj,anchor||null);row.insertBefore(enh,anchor||null);
    // Move the existing Chroma Key / Extract Audio tools beside Adjust/Enhance, in this same row.
    const chroma=$('clipChromaTool'),audio=$('clipExtractAudioTool'),forward=$('clipForwardTool');
    if(forward){if(chroma)row.insertBefore(chroma,forward);if(audio)row.insertBefore(audio,forward);}
    const obsolete=$('ethvelleLocalVideoTools');if(obsolete)obsolete.remove();
  }
  function esc(str){return String(str==null?'':str).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));}
  function renderAdjustmentsPanel(){
    const host=$('evltAdjustmentsPanel');if(!host)return;
    const target=visualTarget();
    if(!target){host.innerHTML='<div class="text-editor-controls"></div>';return;}
    if(!target.model.adjustments)target.model.adjustments={...defaults};
    const a=normalizeAdj(target.model.adjustments);
    const fields=[['brightness','Brightness',0,200,1,'%'],['contrast','Contrast',0,200,1,'%'],['saturation','Saturation',0,200,1,'%'],['hue','Hue',-180,180,1,'°'],['blur','Blur',0,12,.5,'px'],['grayscale','Grayscale',0,100,1,'%'],['sepia','Sepia',0,100,1,'%']];
    const perspVals={x:Math.round(clamp(target.model.mediaPerspectiveX||0,-55,55)),y:Math.round(clamp(target.model.mediaPerspectiveY||0,-55,55))};
    const perspCard=(axis,label,val)=>`<div class="evv-slider-card evlt-adjust-slider"><div class="evv-slider-head"><label for="evAdj_persp${axis}">${label}</label><output id="evAdj_persp${axis}Val">${val}°</output></div><input type="range" id="evAdj_persp${axis}" min="-55" max="55" step="1" value="${val}" style="--evv-progress:${((val+55)/110)*100}%" oninput="window.ethvelleSetMediaPerspective('${axis.toLowerCase()}',this.value);this.style.setProperty('--evv-progress',((Number(this.value)+55)/110)*100+'%')"></div>`;
    const perspHtml=`<div class="evlt-persp-heading" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px;margin-top:6px"><b style="font-size:13px">Perspective</b></div>${perspCard('X','Horizontal',perspVals.x)}${perspCard('Y','Vertical',perspVals.y)}`;
    host.innerHTML=`<div class="text-editor-controls evlt-controls">${fields.map(([k,label,min,max,step,unit])=>{const pct=((Number(a[k])-min)/(max-min))*100;return `<div class="evv-slider-card evlt-adjust-slider"><div class="evv-slider-head"><label for="evAdj_${k}">${label}</label><output id="evAdj_${k}Val">${a[k]}${unit}</output></div><input type="range" id="evAdj_${k}" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step}" value="${a[k]}" style="--evv-progress:${pct}%" oninput="window.ethvelleSetAdjustment('${k}',this.value);this.style.setProperty('--evv-progress',((Number(this.value)-(${min}))/((${max})-(${min})))*100+'%')"></div>`;}).join('')}${perspHtml}<button class="evlt-secondary" onclick="window.ethvelleResetAdjustments()">Reset</button></div>`;
    updateTargetFilter(target);
  }
  window.ethvelleSetAdjustment=function(key,value){
    const target=visualTarget();if(!target)return;if(!target.model.adjustments)target.model.adjustments={...defaults};
    const ranges={brightness:[0,200],contrast:[0,200],saturation:[0,200],hue:[-180,180],blur:[0,12],grayscale:[0,100],sepia:[0,100]};if(!ranges[key])return;
    target.model.adjustments[key]=clamp(value,...ranges[key]);
    const unit=key==='hue'?'°':key==='blur'?'px':'%';const text=target.model.adjustments[key]+unit;
    const out=$('evAdj_'+key+'Val');if(out)out.textContent=text;
    updateTargetFilter(target);clearTimeout(window.__ethvelleAdjustmentPersistTimer);window.__ethvelleAdjustmentPersistTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});},420);
  };
  function applyPerspectiveLive(target){
    if(!target||!target.model||!window.ethvelleStripPerspective)return;
    const suffix=window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix(target.model),els=[];
    if(target.kind==='overlay'){
      const id=target.model.id;
      if(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)els.push(overlayMediaEls[id]);
      if(typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'&&overlayChromaCanvases)els.push(overlayChromaCanvases[id]);
    }else els.push($('bgImgLayer'));
    els.forEach(el=>{
      if(!el||!el.style)return;
      const base=window.ethvelleStripPerspective(el.style.transform);
      if(!base)return; /* no base transform yet: the next render will add the perspective */
      const next=base+suffix;if(el.style.transform!==next)el.style.transform=next;
    });
  }
  window.ethvelleSetMediaPerspective=function(axis,value){
    const target=visualTarget();if(!target)return;
    const key=axis==='y'?'mediaPerspectiveY':'mediaPerspectiveX';
    target.model[key]=Math.round(clamp(value,-55,55));
    const out=$('evAdj_persp'+(axis==='y'?'Y':'X')+'Val');if(out)out.textContent=target.model[key]+'°';
    applyPerspectiveLive(target);
    clearTimeout(window.__ethvellePerspectivePersistTimer);
    window.__ethvellePerspectivePersistTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});},420);
  };
  window.ethvelleResetAdjustments=function(){const target=visualTarget();if(!target)return;target.model.adjustments={...defaults};target.model.mediaPerspectiveX=0;target.model.mediaPerspectiveY=0;applyPerspectiveLive(target);renderAdjustmentsPanel();updateTargetFilter(target);if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();};
  window.ethvelleOpenAdjustments=function(){if(!visualTarget())return;window.openPanel('evltAdjustments');};

  function enhancementCss(amount){const n=clamp(amount,0,100);return `brightness(${100+n*.015}%) contrast(${100+n*.18}%) saturate(${100+n*.12}%)`;}
  function setCompare(percent){
    comparePercent=clamp(percent,0,100);const after=$('evltAfterLayer'),wipe=$('evltCompareWipe'),range=$('evltCompare');
    if(after)after.style.clipPath=`inset(0 ${100-comparePercent}% 0 0)`;if(wipe)wipe.style.left=comparePercent+'%';if(range)range.value=String(comparePercent);
  }
  function syncEnhancePreview(){
    const before=$('evltEnhanceBeforeVideo'),after=$('evltEnhanceAfterVideo');
    if(before&&after&&before.readyState>=1&&after.readyState>=1){try{if(Math.abs(before.currentTime-after.currentTime)>.12)after.currentTime=before.currentTime;}catch(e){}}
  }
  function renderEnhancePanel(){
    const host=$('evltEnhancePanel');if(!host)return;
    const target=enhanceTarget();
    if(!target){host.innerHTML='<div class="text-editor-controls"></div>';return;}
    const model=target.model;migrateLegacyEnhanceDefault(model);const amount=clamp(model.enhanceAmount==null?0:model.enhanceAmount,0,100);if(model.enhanceAmount==null)model.enhanceAmount=0;
    const isVideo=target.type==='video',src=esc(target.src),tag=(id)=>isVideo?`<video id="${id}" src="${src}" muted playsinline preload="metadata"></video>`:`<img src="${src}" alt="">`;
    host.innerHTML=`<div class="text-editor-controls evlt-controls">
      <div class="evlt-compare" id="evltEnhanceCompare">
        <div class="evlt-compare-media">${tag('evltEnhanceBeforeVideo')}</div>
        <div class="evlt-compare-after" id="evltAfterLayer"><div class="evlt-compare-media">${tag('evltEnhanceAfterVideo')}</div></div>
        <span class="evlt-compare-tag before">Before</span><span class="evlt-compare-tag after">After</span><span class="evlt-compare-wipe" id="evltCompareWipe"></span>
      </div>
      <div class="evlt-compare-range-wrap"><input id="evltCompare" class="evlt-compare-range" type="range" min="0" max="100" value="${comparePercent}" aria-label="Before and after comparison" oninput="window.ethvelleSetEnhanceCompare(this.value)"></div>
      <div class="evlt-field"><div class="evlt-field-head"><label for="evltEnhanceAmount">Enhance amount</label><output id="evltEnhanceAmountValue">${amount}%</output></div><div class="text-range-line"><input id="evltEnhanceAmount" type="range" min="0" max="100" step="1" value="${amount}" oninput="window.ethvelleSetEnhanceAmount(this.value)"></div></div>
      <div class="evlt-field"><div class="evlt-field-head"><label for="evltEnhanceQuality">Output quality</label></div><select id="evltEnhanceQuality"><option value="1080">1080p</option><option value="1440">1440p</option><option value="2160">4K</option></select></div>
      <div class="evlt-progress" id="evltEnhanceProgress"><i id="evltEnhanceProgressFill"></i></div><div class="evlt-status" id="evltEnhanceStatus" role="status"></div>
      <button id="evltEnhanceStart" class="evlt-primary" onclick="window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance()">Enhance & Export</button>
    </div>`;
    const before=$('evltEnhanceBeforeVideo'),after=$('evltEnhanceAfterVideo');
    [before,after].forEach(v=>{if(v){v.muted=true;v.defaultMuted=true;v.volume=0;v.playsInline=true;v.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',syncEnhancePreview);v.addEventListener('timeupdate',syncEnhancePreview);}});
    const baseFilter=adjCss(model.adjustments,0);if($('evltEnhanceCompare'))$('evltEnhanceCompare').querySelector('.evlt-compare-media').style.filter=baseFilter;
    if($('evltAfterLayer'))$('evltAfterLayer').style.filter=adjCss(model.adjustments,amount);
    setCompare(comparePercent);updateTargetFilter(target);
  }
  window.ethvelleSetEnhanceCompare=function(v){setCompare(v);};
  window.ethvelleSetEnhanceAmount=function(value){
    const target=enhanceTarget();if(!target)return;const amount=clamp(value,0,100);target.model.enhanceAmount=amount;target.model.__evltEnhanceUserSet=true;
    const out=$('evltEnhanceAmountValue');if(out)out.textContent=amount+'%';
    const after=$('evltAfterLayer');if(after)after.style.filter=adjCss(target.model.adjustments,amount);
    updateTargetFilter(target);if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
  };
  function setEnhanceStatus(text,pct){const el=$('evltEnhanceStatus');if(el)el.textContent=text||'';const bar=$('evltEnhanceProgress'),fill=$('evltEnhanceProgressFill');if(bar)bar.style.display=pct==null?'none':'block';if(fill&&pct!=null)fill.style.width=clamp(pct,0,100)+'%';}
  window.ethvelleOpenEnhance=function(){if(!enhanceTarget())return;comparePercent=50;window.openPanel('evltEnhance');};
  function downloadBlob(blob,name){const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),60000);}
  function outputSize(iw,ih,quality){const ratio=iw/ih;let w,h;if(ratio>=1){h=quality;w=Math.round(quality*ratio);}else{w=quality;h=Math.round(quality/ratio);}return {w:Math.max(2,w-(w%2)),h:Math.max(2,h-(h%2))};}

  async function exportEnhancedImage(target,quality,amount){
    const img=new Image();img.crossOrigin='anonymous';img.decoding='async';
    await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{img.onload=resolve;img.onerror=()=>reject(new Error('Image cannot be processed by this browser.'));img.src=target.src;if(img.complete&&img.naturalWidth)resolve();});
    const {w,h}=outputSize(img.naturalWidth||img.width,img.naturalHeight||img.height,quality);
    const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=w;cv.height=h;const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');if(!ctx)throw new Error('Could not create image canvas.');
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';ctx.filter=enhancementCss(amount);ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
    const blob=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>cv.toBlob(b=>b?resolve(b):reject(new Error('Image export failed.')),'image/png'));
    const base=String(target.model.name||'ethvelle-image').replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'').replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/gi,'_')||'ethvelle-image';
    downloadBlob(blob,`${base}-${quality}p-enhanced.png`);setEnhanceStatus(`${w} × ${h}`,100);
  }
  window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance=async function(){
    if(enhanceRunning)return;const target=enhanceTarget();if(!target)return;
    const quality=Number($('evltEnhanceQuality')?.value)||1080,amount=clamp(target.model.enhanceAmount==null?40:target.model.enhanceAmount,0,100);
    if(target.type==='image'){
      enhanceRunning=true;const btn=$('evltEnhanceStart');if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='Processing…';}
      setEnhanceStatus('',0);
      try{await exportEnhancedImage(target,quality,amount);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Enhanced image exported',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1800});}
      catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle image enhancement:',err);setEnhanceStatus(err&&err.message?err.message:'Image export failed.',null);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Image export failed',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2000});}
      finally{enhanceRunning=false;const b=$('evltEnhanceStart');if(b){b.disabled=false;b.textContent='Enhance & Export';}}
      return;
    }
    if(typeof MediaRecorder==='undefined'||!HTMLCanvasElement.prototype.captureStream){setEnhanceStatus('');if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Video export is not supported in this browser',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2000});return;}
    const clip=target.model;if(target.type!=='video')return;
    const token=++enhanceRunToken;enhanceRunning=true;const btn=$('evltEnhanceStart');if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='Processing…';}
    setEnhanceStatus('',0);
    const video=document.createElement('video');video.preload='auto';video.playsInline=true;video.crossOrigin='anonymous';video.volume=0;video.muted=false;
    let sourceStream=null,outStream=null,recorder=null,raf=0,watchdog=0,stopped=false,chunks=[];
    try{
      video.src=target.src;
      await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{let done=false;const finish=(err)=>{if(done)return;done=true;video.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata',loaded);video.removeEventListener('error',failed);err?reject(err):resolve();};const loaded=()=>finish();const failed=()=>finish(new Error('Video cannot be processed. Check the source URL CORS access or use a local video file.'));video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',loaded,{once:true});video.addEventListener('error',failed,{once:true});video.load();if(video.readyState>=1)finish();});
      if(!video.videoWidth||!video.videoHeight||!Number.isFinite(video.duration)||video.duration<=0)throw new Error('Video has no readable frames or duration.');
      const {w,h}=outputSize(video.videoWidth,video.videoHeight,quality);
      const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=w;canvas.height=h;const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:false});if(!ctx)throw new Error('Could not create video canvas.');ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';
      if(typeof video.captureStream==='function')sourceStream=video.captureStream();else if(typeof video.mozCaptureStream==='function')sourceStream=video.mozCaptureStream();
      let sourceFps=30;try{const track=sourceStream&&sourceStream.getVideoTracks&&sourceStream.getVideoTracks()[0],settings=track&&track.getSettings?track.getSettings():null,detected=Number(settings&&settings.frameRate);if(Number.isFinite(detected)&&detected>=1&&detected<=120)sourceFps=detected;}catch(_e){}
      outStream=canvas.captureStream(sourceFps);
      if(sourceStream)sourceStream.getAudioTracks().forEach(t=>outStream.addTrack(t));
      const mime=['video/webm;codecs=vp9,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp8,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp9','video/webm;codecs=vp8','video/webm'].find(m=>!MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported||MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(m));if(!mime)throw new Error('No supported video format.');
      recorder=new MediaRecorder(outStream,{mimeType:mime,videoBitsPerSecond:quality>=2160?18000000:quality>=1440?12000000:8000000});
      const stopPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{recorder.ondataavailable=e=>{if(e.data&&e.data.size)chunks.push(e.data);};recorder.onerror=e=>reject(e.error||new Error('Video recording failed.'));recorder.onstop=resolve;});
      recorder.start(250);await video.play();
      const duration=video.duration;let lastDraw=0;
      const stopOnce=()=>{if(stopped)return;stopped=true;if(raf)cancelAnimationFrame(raf);if(watchdog)clearTimeout(watchdog);try{if(recorder&&recorder.state!=='inactive')recorder.stop();}catch(e){}};
      watchdog=setTimeout(()=>stopOnce(),Math.ceil(duration*1000)+15000);
      const draw=now=>{
        if(token!==enhanceRunToken||stopped)return;
        if(video.readyState>=2&&now-lastDraw>=1000/sourceFps){lastDraw=now;try{ctx.save();ctx.filter=enhancementCss(amount);ctx.drawImage(video,0,0,w,h);ctx.restore();}catch(e){stopOnce();return;}const pct=clamp(video.currentTime/duration*100,0,99);setEnhanceStatus(`${Math.floor(pct)}%`,pct);}
        if(video.ended||video.currentTime>=duration-.03){stopOnce();return;}raf=requestAnimationFrame(draw);
      };
      raf=requestAnimationFrame(draw);await stopPromise;if(!chunks.length)throw new Error('No video output was created.');
      const blob=new Blob(chunks,{type:recorder.mimeType||'video/webm'});if(!blob.size)throw new Error('The video output is empty.');
      const base=String(clip.name||'ethvelle-video').replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'').replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/gi,'_')||'ethvelle-video';downloadBlob(blob,`${base}-${quality}p-enhanced.webm`);setEnhanceStatus('100%',100);
      if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Enhanced video exported',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1800});
    }catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle local enhancement:',err);setEnhanceStatus(err&&err.message?err.message:'Export failed.',null);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Video export failed',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2000});}
    finally{
      if(raf)cancelAnimationFrame(raf);if(watchdog)clearTimeout(watchdog);try{if(recorder&&recorder.state!=='inactive')recorder.stop();}catch(e){}
      try{video.pause();video.removeAttribute('src');video.load();}catch(e){}try{if(sourceStream)sourceStream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());}catch(e){}try{if(outStream)outStream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());}catch(e){}
      enhanceRunning=false;const b=$('evltEnhanceStart');if(b){b.disabled=false;b.textContent='Enhance & Export';}
    }
  };

  // Keep all four tools in the same selected-clip row and reuse the site's established slide-up panel.
  try{panelContent.evltAdjustments={title:'Adjust',body:'<div id="evltAdjustmentsPanel"></div>'};panelContent.evltEnhance={title:'Enhance',body:'<div id="evltEnhancePanel"></div>'};}catch(e){}
  const oldOpenPanel=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof oldOpenPanel==='function'&&!oldOpenPanel.__evltV2){
    const wrapped=function(key){const result=oldOpenPanel.apply(this,arguments);const panel=$('toolPanel');if(panel){panel.classList.toggle('evlt-expanded-panel',['evltAdjustments','evltEnhance','chroma'].includes(key));panel.classList.toggle('evlt-chroma-compact-panel',key==='chroma');}if(key==='evltAdjustments')renderAdjustmentsPanel();if(key==='evltEnhance')renderEnhancePanel();return result;};wrapped.__evltV2=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
  const oldClosePanel=window.closePanel;
  if(typeof oldClosePanel==='function'&&!oldClosePanel.__evltV2){const wrapped=function(){const result=oldClosePanel.apply(this,arguments);$('toolPanel')?.classList.remove('evlt-expanded-panel','evlt-chroma-compact-panel');document.querySelectorAll('#evltEnhancePanel video').forEach(v=>{try{v.pause();v.removeAttribute('src');v.load();}catch(e){}});return result;};wrapped.__evltV2=true;window.closePanel=wrapped;}

  // Video timeline thumbnails are owned by the lazy, shared sequential decoder below.
  // No per-tile <video> fallback is created: ten full-length decoders per clip would
  // exceed the three-decoder budget on long sources.
  const oldRenderOverlayLayers=window.renderOverlayLayers;
  if(typeof oldRenderOverlayLayers==='function'&&!oldRenderOverlayLayers.__evltV2Filmstrip){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldRenderOverlayLayers.apply(this,arguments);refreshToolVisibility();return result;};
    wrapped.__evltV2Filmstrip=true;window.renderOverlayLayers=wrapped;
  }
  const oldAddAdminAsset=window.addAdminAssetToProject;
  if(typeof oldAddAdminAsset==='function'&&!oldAddAdminAsset.__evltV2Filmstrip){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldAddAdminAsset.apply(this,arguments);try{if(window.__evTimelineVideoThumbs)window.__evTimelineVideoThumbs.schedule();}catch(_e){}return result;};
    wrapped.__evltV2Filmstrip=true;window.addAdminAssetToProject=wrapped;
  }
  const oldApplyBackground=window.applyBackground;
  if(typeof oldApplyBackground==='function'&&!oldApplyBackground.__evltV2Filter){const wrapped=function(){const result=oldApplyBackground.apply(this,arguments);let bg=null;try{bg=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec==='number'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0):null;}catch(e){}if(bg&&!bg.__evltEnhanceUserSet&&Number(bg.enhanceAmount)===40)bg.enhanceAmount=0;const img=$('bgImgLayer');if(img)img.style.filter=bg?adjCss(bg.adjustments,bg.enhanceAmount||0):'none';return result;};wrapped.__evltV2Filter=true;window.applyBackground=wrapped;}
  const oldRenderMedia=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof oldRenderMedia==='function'&&!oldRenderMedia.__evltV2Filter){const wrapped=function(){const result=oldRenderMedia.apply(this,arguments);try{overlays.forEach(ov=>{if(!ov)return;if(!ov.__evltEnhanceUserSet&&Number(ov.enhanceAmount)===40)ov.enhanceAmount=0;const el=overlayMediaEls[ov.id];if(el)el.style.filter=adjCss(ov.adjustments,ov.enhanceAmount||0);const cv=overlayChromaCanvases[ov.id];if(cv)cv.style.filter=adjCss(ov.adjustments,ov.enhanceAmount||0);});}catch(e){}return result;};wrapped.__evltV2Filter=true;window.renderOverlayMedia=wrapped;}
  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__evltV2Tools){const wrapped=function(){const result=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);refreshToolVisibility();return result;};wrapped.__evltV2Tools=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;}
  const oldSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect==='function'&&!oldSelect.__evltV2Tools){const wrapped=function(){const result=oldSelect.apply(this,arguments);refreshToolVisibility();return result;};wrapped.__evltV2Tools=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;}
  const oldDeselect=window.deselectAllClips;
  if(typeof oldDeselect==='function'&&!oldDeselect.__evltV2Tools){const wrapped=function(){const result=oldDeselect.apply(this,arguments);refreshToolVisibility();return result;};wrapped.__evltV2Tools=true;window.deselectAllClips=wrapped;}
  function boot(){ensureToolbar();refreshToolVisibility();try{if(window.__evTimelineVideoThumbs)window.__evTimelineVideoThumbs.schedule();}catch(_e){}}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>



<script id="ethvelle-v3-chroma-color-picker">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const presetColors=['#FFFFFF','#000000','#A3A3A3','#414141','#FFC5CF','#FA847C','#FF4E57','#FF174D','#D70D20','#FF7B24','#FFD43B','#B5E53B','#25C98A','#16D9D0','#13A7FF','#2F5CE8','#605DFF','#875BFA','#D946EF'];
  let activeHex='#00FF00',pickerDraftHex=null,hue=120,saturation=100,lightness=50;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  function safeHex(v){return /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(String(v||''))?String(v).toUpperCase():'#00FF00';}
  function toHex(h,s,l){s/=100;l/=100;const k=n=>(n+h/30)%12,a=s*Math.min(l,1-l),f=n=>{const c=k(n);return l-a*Math.max(-1,Math.min(c-3,Math.min(9-c,1)));};return '#'+[f(0),f(8),f(4)].map(v=>Math.round(v*255).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();}
  function toHsl(hex){const h=safeHex(hex).slice(1),r=parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16)/255,g=parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16)/255,b=parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16)/255,max=Math.max(r,g,b),min=Math.min(r,g,b);let hh=0,ss=0,ll=(max+min)/2,d=max-min;if(d){ss=d/(1-Math.abs(2*ll-1));switch(max){case r:hh=((g-b)/d)%6;break;case g:hh=(b-r)/d+2;break;default:hh=(r-g)/d+4;}hh*=60;if(hh<0)hh+=360;}return {h:hh,s:ss*100,l:ll*100};}
  function getSavedColors(){try{const seen=new Set();return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ethvelleCustomTextColors')||'[]').filter(c=>/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(c)).map(c=>c.toUpperCase()).filter(c=>seen.has(c)?false:(seen.add(c),true)).slice(0,30);}catch(e){return [];}}
  function persistNewColor(hex){const c=safeHex(hex);if(c==='#00FF00'||presetColors.includes(c))return;const saved=getSavedColors();if(saved.includes(c))return;try{localStorage.setItem('ethvelleCustomTextColors',JSON.stringify([c,...saved].slice(0,30)));}catch(e){}}
  function getClip(){try{return typeof getSelectedVideoOverlay==='function'?getSelectedVideoOverlay():null;}catch(e){return null;}}
  function ensureActiveChromaSwatch(hex){
    const host=$('evltChromaPalette');if(!host)return null;const color=safeHex(hex);
    let swatch=[...host.querySelectorAll('.evlt-chroma-swatch[data-color]')].find(b=>safeHex(b.dataset.color)===color);
    if(!swatch){
      swatch=document.createElement('button');swatch.type='button';swatch.className='evlt-chroma-swatch';swatch.dataset.color=color;
      swatch.style.backgroundColor=color;swatch.title=color;swatch.setAttribute('aria-label',color);
      swatch.addEventListener('click',()=>window.ethvelleSetChromaPaletteColor(color,swatch));
      const custom=host.querySelector('.evlt-chroma-custom');host.insertBefore(swatch,custom||null);
    }
    host.querySelectorAll('.evlt-chroma-swatch').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',safeHex(b.dataset.color)===color));
    return swatch;
  }
  function applyToClip(hex){
    const clip=getClip();if(!clip)return;activeHex=safeHex(hex);persistNewColor(activeHex);window.updateChromaSetting('color',activeHex);
    const c=$('evltChromaCurrent');if(c)c.style.background=activeHex;ensureActiveChromaSwatch(activeHex);
  }
  function renderPalette(){
    const host=$('evltChromaPalette');if(!host)return;const clip=getClip(),current=safeHex(clip&&clip.chromaKey&&clip.chromaKey.color||'#00FF00');
    persistNewColor(current);const colors=[...new Set([...getSavedColors(),'#00FF00',...presetColors])];
    host.innerHTML=colors.map(c=>`<button type="button" class="evlt-chroma-swatch ${c===current?'active':''}" style="background:${c}" data-color="${c}" aria-label="${c}" title="${c}" onclick="window.ethvelleSetChromaPaletteColor('${c}',this)"></button>`).join('')+`<button type="button" class="evlt-chroma-custom" aria-label="Custom color" title="Custom color" onclick="window.openEthvelleChromaColorPicker()"></button>`;
    const preview=$('evltChromaCurrent');if(preview)preview.style.background=current;ensureActiveChromaSwatch(current);
  }
  window.ethvelleSetChromaPaletteColor=function(hex,btn){applyToClip(hex);const host=$('evltChromaPalette');host?.querySelectorAll('.evlt-chroma-swatch').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',safeHex(b.dataset.color)===safeHex(hex)));};
  window.renderChromaKeyPanel=function(){
    const host=$('chromaPanelBody');if(!host)return;const clip=getClip();
    if(!clip){host.innerHTML='<div class="chroma-controls"></div>';return;}
    if(!clip.chromaKey)clip.chromaKey={enabled:false,color:'#00FF00',tolerance:75,softness:24};
    const cfg=clip.chromaKey,tol=Math.max(10,Math.min(180,Number(cfg.tolerance)||75)),soft=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number.isFinite(Number(cfg.softness))?Number(cfg.softness):24));
    host.innerHTML=`<div class="chroma-controls evlt-compact-controls">
      <label class="chroma-enable-row evlt-chroma-enable-row"><span>Chroma Key</span><input class="evlt-chroma-check" type="checkbox" ${cfg.enabled?'checked':''} onchange="setChromaEnabled(this.checked)" aria-label="Enable chroma key"></label>
      <div class="evlt-chroma-color-line"><div class="evlt-chroma-color-top"><label>Key color</label><span id="evltChromaCurrent" class="evlt-chroma-current" style="background:${safeHex(cfg.color)}"></span></div><div id="evltChromaPalette" class="evlt-chroma-palette"></div></div>
      <div class="chroma-range evlt-chroma-range"><div class="chroma-range-top"><label for="chromaTolerance">Tolerance</label><output id="chromaToleranceValue">${tol}</output></div><input id="chromaTolerance" type="range" min="10" max="180" step="1" value="${tol}" oninput="updateChromaSetting('tolerance',this.value)"></div>
      <div class="chroma-range evlt-chroma-range"><div class="chroma-range-top"><label for="chromaSoftness">Edge softness</label><output id="chromaSoftnessValue">${soft}</output></div><input id="chromaSoftness" type="range" min="0" max="100" step="1" value="${soft}" oninput="updateChromaSetting('softness',this.value)"></div>
      <button type="button" class="chroma-reset-btn" onclick="resetChromaSettings()">Reset</button>
    </div>`;
    renderPalette();
  };
  function syncPickerBounds(){
    const root=$('ethvelleChromaColorPicker');
    if(!root||!root.classList.contains('active'))return;
    root.style.removeProperty('left');root.style.removeProperty('top');root.style.removeProperty('width');root.style.removeProperty('height');root.style.removeProperty('right');root.style.removeProperty('bottom');
    root.style.setProperty('--evcp-vh',(window.visualViewport?window.visualViewport.height:window.innerHeight)+'px');
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize',syncPickerBounds,{passive:true});
  if(window.visualViewport)window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',syncPickerBounds,{passive:true});
  function renderPicker(){
    let root=$('ethvelleChromaColorPicker');if(!root){root=document.createElement('div');root.id='ethvelleChromaColorPicker';root.className='evcp-root evlt-chroma-picker-overlay';document.body.appendChild(root);root.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===root)window.closeEthvelleChromaColorPicker();});}
    root.classList.add('evlt-chroma-picker-overlay');
    const current=safeHex(pickerDraftHex||activeHex),hsl=toHsl(current);pickerDraftHex=current;hue=hsl.h;saturation=hsl.s;lightness=hsl.l;
    root.innerHTML=`<div class="evcp-sheet"><div class="evcp-grabber"></div><div class="evcp-head"><div><h3>Custom Color</h3></div><button type="button" class="evcp-close" aria-label="Close" onclick="window.closeEthvelleChromaColorPicker()">×</button></div><div class="evcp-main"><div id="evckWheel" class="evcp-wheel" role="slider" aria-label="Color wheel" tabindex="0" style="--picked-color:${current}"></div><div class="evcp-preview-row"><span id="evckPreview" class="evcp-preview-chip" style="background-color:${current}"></span><label for="evckHex">HEX</label><input id="evckHex" class="evcp-hex" value="${current}" maxlength="7" spellcheck="false" oninput="window.setEthvelleChromaPickerHex(this.value)"></div><div class="evcp-field"><label for="evckLight">Lightness <output id="evckLightVal">${Math.round(lightness)}%</output></label><input id="evckLight" type="range" min="5" max="95" value="${Math.round(lightness)}" oninput="window.setEthvelleChromaPickerLightness(this.value)"></div></div><button type="button" class="evcp-done" onclick="window.applyEthvelleChromaCustomColor()">Done</button></div>`;
    root.classList.add('active');
    syncPickerBounds();requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(syncPickerBounds));
    const wheel=$('evckWheel');let dragging=false;
    function setWheel(e){const r=wheel.getBoundingClientRect(),dx=e.clientX-(r.left+r.width/2),dy=e.clientY-(r.top+r.height/2),radius=Math.max(1,Math.min(r.width,r.height)/2),distance=Math.min(radius,Math.hypot(dx,dy));hue=(Math.atan2(dy,dx)*180/Math.PI+90+360)%360;saturation=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,distance/radius*100));drawPickerColor();}
    wheel.onpointerdown=e=>{dragging=true;wheel.setPointerCapture&&wheel.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);setWheel(e);e.preventDefault();};wheel.onpointermove=e=>{if(dragging)setWheel(e);};wheel.onpointerup=wheel.onpointercancel=()=>{dragging=false;};wheel.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='ArrowRight')hue=(hue+4)%360;else if(e.key==='ArrowLeft')hue=(hue+356)%360;else return;drawPickerColor();};
    drawPickerColor();
  }
  function drawPickerColor(){pickerDraftHex=toHex(hue,saturation,lightness);const wheel=$('evckWheel'),hex=$('evckHex'),prev=$('evckPreview'),lv=$('evckLightVal');if(wheel){const ang=(hue-90)*Math.PI/180,rad=42*saturation/100;wheel.style.setProperty('--picked-color',pickerDraftHex);wheel.style.setProperty('--picker-x',(50+rad*Math.cos(ang))+'%');wheel.style.setProperty('--picker-y',(50+rad*Math.sin(ang))+'%');}if(hex&&hex!==document.activeElement)hex.value=pickerDraftHex;if(prev)prev.style.backgroundColor=pickerDraftHex;if(lv)lv.textContent=Math.round(lightness)+'%';}
  window.openEthvelleChromaColorPicker=function(){const clip=getClip();if(!clip)return;pickerDraftHex=safeHex(clip.chromaKey&&clip.chromaKey.color||'#00FF00');renderPicker();};
  window.closeEthvelleChromaColorPicker=function(){const root=$('ethvelleChromaColorPicker');if(root)root.classList.remove('active');pickerDraftHex=null;};
  window.setEthvelleChromaPickerHex=function(v){if(!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(String(v||'')))return;pickerDraftHex=safeHex(v);const h=toHsl(pickerDraftHex);hue=h.h;saturation=h.s;lightness=h.l;drawPickerColor();};
  window.setEthvelleChromaPickerLightness=function(v){lightness=Math.max(5,Math.min(95,Number(v)||50));drawPickerColor();};
  window.applyEthvelleChromaCustomColor=function(){const color=safeHex(pickerDraftHex||activeHex);if(!getClip())return;applyToClip(color);activeHex=color;try{persistNewColor(color);}catch(e){}window.closeEthvelleChromaColorPicker();renderPalette();};
  window.saveEthvelleChromaCustomColor=function(){let arr=getSavedColors();if(!arr.includes(activeHex))arr=[activeHex,...arr].slice(0,30);try{localStorage.setItem('ethvelleCustomTextColors',JSON.stringify(arr));}catch(e){}renderPalette();};
  // Rebuild the swatch row after choosing Reset and keep panel values in sync.
  const oldReset=window.resetChromaSettings;
  if(typeof oldReset==='function'&&!oldReset.__evltV3){const wrapped=function(){const r=oldReset.apply(this,arguments);renderPalette();return r;};wrapped.__evltV3=true;window.resetChromaSettings=wrapped;}
  const oldOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function'&&!oldOpen.__evltChromaPalette){const wrapped=function(key){const r=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='chroma')window.renderChromaKeyPanel();return r;};wrapped.__evltChromaPalette=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;}
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v5-auto-apply-enhance-watermark">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const neutralAdjustments={brightness:100,contrast:100,saturation:100,hue:0,blur:0,grayscale:0,sepia:0};
  let pendingEnhancement=null,pendingPreviewURL=null;
  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>{v=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(v)?Math.max(min,Math.min(max,v)):min;};
  const unwrap=src=>{let s=String(src||'').trim();const m=s.match(/^url\((.*)\)$/i);return m?m[1].trim().replace(/^['"]|['"]$/g,''):s;};
  function liveTarget(){
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':selectedClip);
    const b=(typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(backgrounds))?backgrounds.find(x=>String(x.id)===id):null;
    if(b)return {kind:'background',model:b,type:'image',src:b.__evltRuntimeEnhancedSrc||unwrap(b.src)};
    const ov=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays.find(x=>String(x.id)===id):null;
    if(ov&&['video','image'].includes(ov.type))return {kind:'overlay',model:ov,type:ov.type,src:ov.__evltRuntimeEnhancedSrc||unwrap(ov.src)};
    if(!id){let active=null;try{active=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec==='number'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0)):null;}catch(e){}if(active)return {kind:'background',model:active,type:'image',src:active.__evltRuntimeEnhancedSrc||unwrap(active.src)};}
    return null;
  }
  function resolvePendingTarget(pending){
    const saved=pending&&pending.target;if(!saved||!saved.model||saved.model.id==null)return null;
    const id=String(saved.model.id);
    const list=saved.kind==='overlay'?(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]):saved.kind==='background'?(Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[]):[];
    const model=list.find(item=>item&&String(item.id)===id&&(saved.kind!=='overlay'||item.type===saved.type));
    if(!model)return null;
    return {...saved,model,src:model.__evltRuntimeEnhancedSrc||unwrap(model.src)};
  }
  function qualityLabel(q){return Number(q)>=2160?'ULTRA HD':Number(q)>=1440?'QHD':'FULL HD';}
  function filterFor(model,amount){
    try{if(typeof window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter==='function')return window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(model&&model.adjustments,0);}catch(e){}
    return 'none';
  }
  function roundedRect(ctx,x,y,w,h,r){
    r=Math.min(r,w/2,h/2);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+r,y);ctx.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,r);ctx.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,r);ctx.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,r);ctx.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,r);ctx.closePath();
  }
  function drawQualityWatermark(ctx,w,h,label){
    const scale=Math.max(.7,Math.min(w,h)/720),font=Math.max(15,Math.round(17*scale)),padX=Math.round(font*.62),padY=Math.round(font*.38),margin=Math.round(font*.65),radius=Math.round(font*.32);
    ctx.save();ctx.font=`800 ${font}px Inter, Arial, sans-serif`;ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    const tw=ctx.measureText(label).width,bw=Math.ceil(tw+padX*2),bh=Math.ceil(font+padY*2),x=w-margin-bw,y=h-margin-bh;
    roundedRect(ctx,x,y,bw,bh,radius);ctx.fillStyle='rgba(8,10,16,.68)';ctx.fill();ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.72)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,scale);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle='#FFFFFF';ctx.fillText(label,x+padX,y+bh/2);ctx.restore();
  }
  let evltEnhancedDbPromise=null;
  function openEnhancedDb(){
    if(!('indexedDB' in window))return Promise.reject(new Error('IndexedDB unavailable'));
    if(!evltEnhancedDbPromise)evltEnhancedDbPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const req=indexedDB.open('ethvelle-enhanced-media-v1',1);req.onupgradeneeded=()=>{const db=req.result;if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains('assets'))db.createObjectStore('assets');};req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('IndexedDB open failed'));});
    return evltEnhancedDbPromise;
  }
  function mediaStamp(src){const s=String(src||'');let h=2166136261;const stride=Math.max(1,Math.floor(s.length/1600));for(let i=0;i<s.length;i+=stride){h^=s.charCodeAt(i);h=Math.imul(h,16777619);}return s.length.toString(36)+'-'+(h>>>0).toString(36);}
  async function storeEnhancedMedia(target,blob,quality){
    const projectId=typeof currentProject!=='undefined'&&currentProject&&currentProject.id!=null?String(currentProject.id):'draft';
    const key=[projectId,target.kind,String(target.model.id||'media'),'enhanced'].join(':');
    const stamp=mediaStamp(target.model.deviceMediaKey?'ethvelle-device-media:'+target.model.deviceMediaKey:target.model.src);
    try{const db=await openEnhancedDb();await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('assets','readwrite');tx.objectStore('assets').put({blob,quality:Number(quality),stamp,kind:target.kind,type:target.type},key);tx.oncomplete=resolve;tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('Enhanced media save failed'));tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('Enhanced media save was cancelled'));});return {key,stamp,quality:Number(quality)};}catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle: enhanced output is session-only because local media storage is unavailable.',e);return null;}
  }
  async function loadEnhancedMedia(key){const db=await openEnhancedDb();return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('assets','readonly'),req=tx.objectStore('assets').get(key);req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result||null);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('Enhanced media load failed'));});}
  function defineRuntimeUrl(model,url,quality){const old=model.__evltRuntimeEnhancedSrc;if(old&&old!==url&&String(old).startsWith('blob:')){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(old);}catch(e){}}Object.defineProperty(model,'__evltRuntimeEnhancedSrc',{value:url,writable:true,configurable:true,enumerable:false});Object.defineProperty(model,'__evltRuntimeEnhancedQuality',{value:qualityLabel(quality),writable:true,configurable:true,enumerable:false});}
  // V285: MediaRecorder writes WebM/Matroska without a Duration or a seek index (Cues). Chrome then reports
  // duration=Infinity and the app's "prime" seek (currentTime=1e7) can wedge the <video> in a never-ending seek:
  // the clip stays hidden / frozen while its audio is fine. This rewrites only the container header
  // (adds Duration + Cues, gives Segment/Cluster real sizes) and reuses the encoded frames untouched.
  // Any unexpected structure returns null so the caller keeps the original blob.
  function evltRemuxSeekable(d){
    const L1=[0x1F43B675,0x1C53BB6B,0x1549A966,0x1654AE6B,0x114D9B74,0x1254C367,0x1043A770,0x1941A469];
    const rd=(p,marker)=>{
      if(p>=d.length)throw new Error('eof');
      const b=d[p];let n=1,m=0x80;while(n<=8&&!(b&m)){n++;m>>=1;}
      if(n>8||p+n>d.length)throw new Error('vint');
      let v=marker?b:(b&(m-1));let all=(b&(m-1))===(m-1);
      for(let i=1;i<n;i++){v=v*256+d[p+i];if(d[p+i]!==0xFF)all=false;}
      return {n:n,v:v,unk:!marker&&all};
    };
    const uint=(s,e)=>{let v=0;for(let i=s;i<e;i++)v=v*256+d[i];return v;};
    const vsize=n=>n<127?1:n<16383?2:n<2097151?3:n<268435455?4:5;
    const wsize=(n,len)=>{const a=new Uint8Array(len);let v=n;for(let i=len-1;i>=0;i--){a[i]=v%256;v=Math.floor(v/256);}a[0]|=(0x80>>(len-1));return a;};
    const concat=arrs=>{let t=0;arrs.forEach(a=>t+=a.length);const o=new Uint8Array(t);let q=0;arrs.forEach(a=>{o.set(a,q);q+=a.length;});return o;};
    const idBytes=id=>{const a=[];let v=id;while(v>0){a.unshift(v%256);v=Math.floor(v/256);}return new Uint8Array(a);};
    const el=(id,payload)=>concat([idBytes(id),wsize(payload.length,vsize(payload.length)),payload]);
    // EBML header
    const h=rd(0,true);if(h.v!==0x1A45DFA3)return null;
    const hs=rd(h.n,false);const ebmlEnd=h.n+hs.n+hs.v;
    const sg=rd(ebmlEnd,true);if(sg.v!==0x18538067)return null;
    const sgs=rd(ebmlEnd+sg.n,false);
    const S=ebmlEnd+sg.n+sgs.n;const segEnd=sgs.unk?d.length:Math.min(d.length,S+sgs.v);
    let info=null,tracks=null;const others=[];const clusters=[];
    let pos=S;
    while(pos<segEnd){
